Our results reveal clear performance hierarchies among different modular configurations and show that carefully optimized designs can significantly outperform naive quantum search. This work establishes a principled benchmark methodology for variational quantum cryptanalysis and positions S-DES as a practical testbed for evaluating quantum attacks on symmetric ciphers in the NISQ era.

</details>


### [110] [Spin bath mediated long-lived coherent oscillations of NV centers in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30146)
*Akshat Rana,Pooja Lamba,Basanta Mistri,Dieter Suter,Siddharth Dhomkar,Rama K. Kamineni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现金刚石NV中心的自旋浴虽然通常导致退相干，但在特定磁场方向（垂直于NV轴）的能量能级反交叉点，会诱发电子和14N核自旋之间的纠缠，使自旋回波包络出现14N核自旋跃迁频率的调制振荡，且该振荡具有长相干时间和高稳定性，为多体物理和量子传感提供了新机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退相干是量子技术中最大的瓶颈，理解并利用原本导致退相干的自旋浴效应，探索其在NV中心系统中产生的新物理现象。

Method: 实验上在金刚石NV中心ensemble上进行电子自旋回波测量，观察回波包络的调制；结合数值模拟验证自旋浴耦合对调制现象的必要性。

Result: 在垂直于NV轴的磁场方向能级反交叉点处，观察到14N核自旋跃迁频率调制的振荡；该振荡的相干时间为平行磁场方向的2-3倍，频率高度稳定且抗环境波动。

Conclusion: 揭示了自旋浴在多体相互作用中可诱导核-电子纠缠的新机制，为多体物理基础研究和量子传感提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Decoherence is the biggest bottleneck in all quantum technologies. For nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, the loss of coherence is caused by the electron and nuclear spin bath of the diamond lattice. Here, we demonstrate that the spin bath - that typically causes decoherence - entangles the spin states of the NV electron and the host $^{14}$N nucleus. The many-body interaction between the $^{14}$N nucleus - electron - bath spins at an energy level anti-crossing occurring for an applied magnetic field orientation perpendicular to the NV axis is responsible for this effect. This is observed experimentally on NV ensembles via electron spin-echo measurements, where the echo envelope is modulated at the frequency of a $^{14}$N nuclear spin transition. Using numerical simulations, we show that the spin bath coupling to the NV centers is essential for observing this modulation. Due to the zero first-order Zeeman effect at the anti-crossing, the observed oscillations have long spin-echo coherence times, 2--3 times those at the parallel magnetic field orientation. The oscillation frequency is highly stable and robust against environmental fluctuations. These findings provide new opportunities for fundamental studies of many-body physics and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum percolation theory for dynamic propagation connectivity of transport networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30218)
*Junxiang Xu,Chence Niu,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Vinayak Dixit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动态传播连通性(DPC)作为衡量交通网络在结构扰动下传播能力的新指标，通过引入量子渗流理论建立谱分析框架，证明DPC在均匀扰动下保持不变、在非均匀扰动下退化，并推导了退化速率与最小特征值间距及异质扰动强度的定量关系，在三个基准网络上验证了相关理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于渗流理论和拓扑连通性的方法只能判断路径是否存在和连通分量是否碎裂，无法捕捉网络拓扑完整但传播能力已显著下降的功能退化问题，亟需一种能刻画网络传播能力退化的新度量。

Method: 将量子渗流理论引入交通网络连通性分析，将受扰动交通网络映射为传播算子系统，建立DPC的谱分析框架；定义时间平均参与指数作为核心量化指标；通过谱理论推导退化速率与最小特征值间距、异质扰动强度的定量关系；在环图上推导DPC解析表达式及二阶扰动近似。

Result: DPC在均匀扰动下保持不变，在异质扰动下退化；建立了退化速率、传播算子最小特征值间距与异质扰动强度之间的定量关系；证明了DPC与代数连通性的分离定理；在三个交通基准网络上的数值实验验证了退化单调性、与代数连通性的分离性以及网络规模对退化的放大效应。

Conclusion: 本文提供了超越拓扑连通性的交通网络韧性评估理论框架，能有效捕捉拓扑完整但传播能力下降的功能退化现象。

Abstract: Connectivity degradation in transport networks under structural disturbance is a central problem in network resilience research. Existing methods rely mainly on percolation theory and topological connectivity measures. They focus on whether paths exist and whether connected components fragment. These approaches cannot capture functional degradation where network topology remains intact but propagation ability has already declined substantially. This paper introduces quantum percolation theory into transport network connectivity analysis and proposes Dynamic Propagation Connectivity (DPC) as a new measure that characterises network propagation ability under disturbance. By mapping a transport network under disturbance into a propagation operator system, this paper establishes a spectral analysis framework for DPC and defines the time-averaged participation index as its core quantification. This paper provides a series of rigorous theoretical results. DPC remains constant under homogeneous disturbance and degrades under heterogeneous disturbance. This paper establishes a quantitative relationship between the degradation rate, the minimum eigenvalue spacing of the propagation operator, and heterogeneous deviation strength. This paper proves a separation theorem between DPC and algebraic connectivity. It derives an analytical expression for DPC and a second-order perturbation approximation on the ring graph. Numerical experiments on three transport benchmark networks verify all theoretical conclusions and confirm degradation monotonicity, separation from algebraic connectivity, and degradation amplification by network size. This paper provides a theoretical framework for transport network resilience assessment that goes beyond topological connectivity.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum percolation based dynamic propagation connectivity for critical-area identification in transport networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30242)
*Junxiang Xu,Chence Niu,Vinayak Dixit,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子渗滤的动态传播连通性(DPC)指标，将链路旅行时间转化为连续传播强度，通过区域退化实验识别交通网络中导致网络传播能力损失最大的关键空间区域。在Sioux Falls基准网络和佛罗里达州六个受飓风伊尔玛影响的公路网络中验证，结果表明DPC识别出的关键区域与传统二元渗滤指标不同，揭示了连续传播退化与二元断裂的不同脆弱性模式，且结果具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通网络韧性分析多关注链路中心性度量或二元渗滤（连通性断裂），但对哪些空间区域导致网络传播能力（而非单纯拓扑连接）最大损失的认识有限。

Method: 基于量子渗滤理论构建DPC度量：将时变链路旅行时间转化为连续传播强度，定义厄米传播算子；通过区域退化实验（降低指定区域所有链路的传播强度）测量DPC损失量，以此评估区域关键性。

Result: Sioux Falls基准网络验证了DPC能识别预定义的结构关键走廊；在佛罗里达网络中，DPC识别的关键区域与链路计数、局部退化、边介数、代数连通度和经典渗滤等方法不同；网络1-4中DPC与经典渗滤排名呈负Spearman相关；稳健性测试（替代时间缩放、退化强度、网格尺寸）的平均排名一致性为0.84-0.96。

Conclusion: 该研究将基于渗滤的交通韧性分析从二元连通性损失拓展到连续传播退化，提供了一个可用于区域监测、应急规划和恢复优先级排序的空间诊断工具。

Abstract: Transport networks often lose functionality through gradual degradation in link operating conditions before topological disconnection occurs. Link-centred and binary percolation measures identify important facilities or connectivity failures, but they provide limited information on which spatial areas cause the largest loss of network-wide propagation capability. This paper develops a Dynamic Propagation Connectivity (DPC) metric based on quantum percolation for critical-area identification in transport networks. Time-varying link travel times are converted into continuous propagation strengths, which define a Hermitian propagation operator at each observation time. Candidate regions are then evaluated by a regional degradation experiment that measures the resulting loss of DPC. The method is applied to a benchmark Sioux Falls network and six Florida road networks during the post-Hurricane Irma disruption and recovery period, using 1,281 five-minute observation times. The benchmark confirms that the regional DPC score identifies a predefined structurally critical corridor. In the Florida networks, the identified critical areas differ from regions selected by link count, local degradation, edge betweenness, algebraic connectivity, and classical percolation. In Networks 1 to 4, DPC and classical percolation rankings have negative Spearman correlations, showing that continuous propagation degradation and binary fragmentation reveal different vulnerability patterns. Robustness tests under alternative travel time scaling, degradation strength, and grid size show stable results, with mean rank agreement between 0.84 and 0.96. The findings extend transport resilience analysis based on percolation from binary connectivity loss to continuous propagation degradation and provide a spatial diagnostic tool for regional monitoring, emergency planning, and recovery prioritisation.

</details>


### [113] [Photonic Violation of Wigner's Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30255)
*Maximilian Rottensteiner,Dorian Schiffer,Tobias Pausch,Alois Mair,Anton Zeilinger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供了一个教学资源，推导了Wigner不等式，描述了量子光学实验装置以验证其违背，并提供了原始实验数据，使学生和教师能够自主分析。测量数据清晰地展示了Wigner不等式的违背，直接说明了量子关联的非经典性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学教学面临挑战，尤其是其理论与经典世界观的冲突。量子关联的反直觉特性通常通过Bell型不等式揭示，而Wigner不等式作为其中特别易于理解的一种测试，基于最小集合论假设。本文旨在通过连接理论、实验和数据分析，为教育工作者提供资源，帮助学生深入理解量子系统并进行真实的科学实践。

Method: 本文采用教学性论文形式，首先推导Wigner不等式，然后描述用于实验违背该不等式的量子光学装置，最后提供原始实验数据供学生和教师自行分析。

Result: 测量数据显示了Wigner不等式的明显违背，直接证明了量子关联的非经典性质。

Conclusion: 通过将理论、实验和数据分析相结合，本文为教育工作者提供了有效的教学资源，能够吸引学生参与真实的科学实践，并培养其对量子系统更深刻的理解。

Abstract: Teaching quantum mechanics is challenging, not least because the theory often conflicts with our classical worldview. Quantum correlations in particular are notoriously counter-intuitive. Their non-classical behavior is typically revealed through Bell-type inequalities. Among these, Wigner's Inequality constitutes a particularly accessible test, as it relies on minimal set-theoretic assumptions. In this pedagogical paper, we derive Wigner's Inequality, describe a quantum-optical setup to experimentally violate it, and provide access to the raw data, enabling students and instructors to perform their own analyses. Our measured data shows clear violations of Wigner's Inequality, directly illustrating the non-classical nature of quantum correlations. By connecting theory, experiment, and data analysis, this paper equips educators with a resource for engaging students in authentic scientific practice and developing a deeper understanding of quantum systems.

</details>


### [114] [Action on the Sphere: An Interfering Mean-Field Propagator for the Bose-Hubbard Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30276)
*Elana F Todd-Miller,Eva-Maria Graefe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Bose-Hubbard二聚体系统中，基于SU(2)相干态和平均场轨迹，构建了一个包含相位信息的初值相干态传播子，并通过时间切片方法精确捕捉了多粒子隧穿效应，能够再现量子击穿和复原动力学。该方法可推广至更一般的SU(2)和SU(M)系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bose-Hubbard系统的多粒子动力学即使在两模情况下也表现出复杂的干涉效应（如复原、击穿和隧穿），而平均场近似虽是最基本的理论描述但无法完全捕捉这些量子效应。本文旨在构建一个超越平均场的简单传播子方法，以更准确地描述多粒子态的完整时间演化。

Method: 基于时间依赖变分原理从SU(2)相干态推导平均场近似，进而构建初值相干态传播子：对平均场轨迹进行求和并保留相应的平均场作用量相位。在此基础上施加时间切片程序，以精确捕捉多粒子隧穿效应。

Result: 该方法能够有效再现量子击穿和复原动力学，时间切片后的传播子准确捕捉了多粒子隧穿效应。分析聚焦于Bose-Hubbard二聚体，但所发展的方法适用于更一般的SU(2)哈密顿量，并可拓展至SU(M)系统。

Conclusion: 本文提出的相干态传播子方法为描述Bose-Hubbard系统多粒子动力学提供了一种简单而有效的近似方案，在保持平均场计算简便性的同时，通过相位求和和时间切片成功地捕捉了关键的量子干涉和隧穿效应，具有良好的可推广性。

Abstract: The Bose-Hubbard system has been studied extensively both theoretically and experimentally, in particular in the context of ultracold atomic gases in optical lattices. Even in the two-mode case the many-particle dynamics display complex interference effects resulting in revival and breakdown phenomena as well as tunnelling. The most basic theoretical description is the mean-field approximation, which can be derived from a time-dependent variational principle assuming the many-particle wave function is an SU(2) coherent state. Here we build on this to construct a simple initial-value coherent state propagator, summing over mean-field trajectories and keeping track of their phases, given by the corresponding mean-field actions. This yields an approximation to the full time-dependent many-particle state, and is able to reproduce breakdown and revival dynamics. Applying a time-slicing procedure on top of this, we are able to accurately capture many-particle tunnelling effects. While in this paper we focus our analysis on the Bose-Hubbard dimer, the methods developed can be applied to more general SU(2) Hamiltonians, and can be extended to SU(M) systems.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Lazy Sampling and Path Recording for Any Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30281)
*Ben Foxman,Alex Lombardi,Fermi Ma,Barak Nehoran,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的路径记录预言机（path-recording oracle），可完美模拟$U(N)$任意闭子群的随机群元素；该预言机以叠加态存储输入-输出对，并通过群张量幂表示的交换子来描述更新，从而透明地记录算法学到的信息。将其应用于$S_N$与$U(N)$的比较，得到了目前最简洁的伪随机酉构造：伪随机排列与随机Clifford的乘积（PC），优于先前的PFC构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法与密码学中的一个核心挑战是：如何推理以随机群元素（如随机函数、置换、酉）为预言机访问的算法？能否高效模拟这些算法？能否确定算法在t次查询后学到了什么？经典工具是惰性采样（lazy sampling），即预言机不提前承诺完整的群元素，而是在查询过程中动态采样部分信息。本文旨在研究量子的惰性采样类比——压缩预言机（compressed oracles / recording oracles），并寻求一个通用、可解释的路径记录预言机。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，定义并分析了一个通用的路径记录预言机（path-recording oracle），可完美模拟$U(N)$的任意闭子群的随机群元素。该预言机在叠加态中存储t个输入-输出对，其更新规则由群张量幂表示的交换子（commutant）描述。该方法建立在Grinko-Yoshida (QIP '26)的工作之上，但后者缺乏清晰的可解释性。通过比较$S_N$和$U(N)$的压缩预言机，得到了伪随机酉（PRU）的新构造：PC = 伪随机排列 × 随机Clifford。

Result: 得到了一个通用且可解释的路径记录预言机，能够完美模拟$U(N)$任意闭子群的随机群元素。基于该预言机，通过比较$S_N$和$U(N)$，得到了目前最简洁的伪随机酉构造——PC（伪随机排列与随机Clifford的乘积），改进了先前的PFC（伪随机函数+伪随机排列+Clifford）构造。

Conclusion: 本文的路径记录预言机为量子查询场景下随机群元素的模拟提供了一个通用、可解释的框架，其通过群张量幂交换子描述的更新机制透明地记录了算法学到的信息。该预言机的一大应用是允许不同群压缩预言机之间的直接比较，从而为伪随机性证明提供新工具。特别地，$S_N$与$U(N)$的比较得到了最简洁的伪随机酉构造。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum algorithms and cryptography is reasoning about algorithms with oracle access to a random group element (e.g. a random function, permutation, or unitary). Can we efficiently simulate such algorithms? Can we determine what they know after t queries? A classical tool for this is lazy sampling: the oracle does not commit to the full group element upfront, but rather samples partial information about it on the fly. We study a quantum analog of lazy sampling: compressed oracles (or recording oracles). These are quantum data structures that allow on-the-fly simulation for quantum queries, originally introduced by Zhandry (CRYPTO '19) for random functions, and generalized to unitaries by Ma-Huang (STOC '25) and permutations by Carolan (STOC '26), and used to great effect in security proofs and lower bounds due to their interpretability.
  We define and analyze a general-purpose and interpretable path-recording oracle, derived from first principles, that perfectly simulates random elements of any closed subgroup of $U(N)$. Our oracle stores, in superposition, t input-output pairs, with updates described in terms of the commutant of the group's tensor power representation. This transparently records the information the algorithm has learned. Our oracle builds on recent work of Grinko-Yoshida (QIP '26), who gave a different general-purpose compressed oracle without clear interpretability.
  One interesting application of our path-recording is allowing direct comparisons between compressed oracles of different groups, giving a new technique for proving pseudorandomness results. For example, comparing $S_N$ and $U(N)$ yields what is arguably the simplest construction to date of pseudorandom unitaries: the product PC of a pseudorandom permutation and a random Clifford, improving on the prior PFC construction (Metger-Poremba-Sinha-Yuen, FOCS '24; Ma-Huang, STOC '25).

</details>


### [116] [Thermometry with multilevel transmon probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30302)
*Antonio Mandarino,Matteo G. A. Paris,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导transmon系统超越二能级近似的量子热力学极限，发现引入更高激发态可增强温度估计精度，并指出标准四次截断势阱存在无界问题，通过引入有界非谐模型评估了热测量精度的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: transmon系统的非简谐性能级结构对热布居和热测量灵敏度有显著影响，但先前研究多局限于二能级（qubit）近似，缺乏对多能级非谐结构如何影响量子温度估计极限的系统理解。

Method: 对比三种互补模型（qubit、谐振子、弱非谐Duffing振子）的量子Fisher信息（QFI），并引入有界非谐模型（受限四次势阱和六次修正项）以解决标准四次截断势阱无下界的问题。

Result: 包含更高激发态的多能级非谐结构可提高可提取的温度信息量，超越qubit探针的性能；标准四次截断势阱存在无界问题，仅支持虚假亚稳态，而有界模型可评估Duffing regime之外的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 为基于transmon的纳米尺度热测量提供了实用指导，阐明了非谐多能级谱在量子温度估计中的作用。

Abstract: Superconducting transmon systems are promising platforms for nanoscale thermometry due to their high sensitivity to environmental fluctuations. Their intrinsic anharmonicity, which is essential for qubit operations, gives rise to a non-equidistant energy spectrum that significantly affects the thermal populations and, consequently, the thermometric sensitivity. In this work, we investigate the ultimate quantum limits of temperature estimation with a transmon beyond the two-level approximation. We compare the thermometric performance of three complementary models: the qubit, a harmonic oscillator and a weakly anharmonic Duffing oscillator, evaluating their corresponding quantum Fisher information (QFI) as a function of the temperature. We show that the multilevel anharmonic structure of the transmon affects its thermometric precision. Indeed, including higher excited states enhances the maximum amount of information that can be extracted about the system temperature, compared to a qubit probe. Furthermore, we address a fundamental limitation of the standard quartic truncation, which yields a potential that is unbounded from below and supports only spurious metastable states. By introducing bounded anharmonic models, namely a confined quartic potential and a sextic correction term, we assess the robustness of the thermometric precision beyond the Duffing regime. Our results provide practical guidelines for transmon-based nanoscale thermometry and clarify the role of the anharmonic multilevel spectrum in quantum temperature estimation.

</details>


### [117] [All-optical switching of continuous-variable entanglement in an absorption-suppressed plasmonic nanodimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30315)
*Elif Ozturk,Mehmet Gunay,Ramazan Sahin,Mehmet Emre Tasgin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种正交等离子体纳米棒二聚体结构，能够同时实现非经典光产生、抑制等离子体损耗和动态可调谐，通过相位锁定控制偏振诱导等离子体折射率增强，驱动探针响应进入近零消光区域，同时调谐局部二次谐波参量相互作用，实现吸收抑制的非线性等离子体光源和量子关联的全光控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚波长量子光子电路元件需要同时满足三个条件：产生非经典光、抑制等离子体损耗、保持动态可调谐，现有技术难以同时满足这三点。

Method: 采用正交等离子体纳米棒二聚体结构，通过相位锁定控制偏振诱导等离子体折射率增强，使探针响应进入近零消光区域，同时调谐局部二次谐波参量相互作用。

Result: 实现了吸收抑制的非线性等离子体光源，展示了可切换的对数负性和单模非经典性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建远低于衍射极限的主动可调谐量子等离子体电路元件建立了新路径。

Abstract: A subwavelength quantum-photonic circuit element should simultaneously generate nonclassical light, suppress plasmonic loss, and remain dynamically tunable. We show that an orthogonal plasmonic nanorod dimer can satisfy all three requirements. A phase-locked control polarization induces plasmonic refractive-index enhancement, driving the probe response toward a near-zero-extinction regime while simultaneously tuning the local second-harmonic parametric interaction. The resulting nonlinear plasmonic source operates in an absorption-suppressed regime and enables all-optical control of quantum correlations. We demonstrate switchable logarithmic negativity and single-mode nonclassicality, establishing a route toward actively tunable quantum-plasmonic circuit elements operating well below the diffraction limit.

</details>


### [118] [Phase-Altered Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking for Compiled Quantum Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30327)
*Simona K. Grigorova,Nikolay V. Vitanov,Boyan T. Torosov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出相位改变交错随机基准测试（PA-IRB）协议，用于检测虚拟相位门是否显著影响交错随机基准测试（IRB）的误差估计。在IBM超导处理器上的Toffoli门案例研究中，Δr在不确定度内与零一致，表明虚拟相位操作不会显著改变IRB误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导处理器中大量非Clifford相位门通过软件帧更新虚拟实现，而非控制脉冲。这引发了一个问题：插入或移除这些虚拟相位是否会可测量地改变IRB误差估计。现有IRB标准保证要求交错门为Clifford门，但虚拟相位操作的存在使得该保证不再显然成立。

Method: 提出PA-IRB配对诊断协议，比较来自同一编译实现的去相位（phase-stripped）和加相位（phase-dressed）Clifford交错门。通过计算Δr = r_d - r_s及其组合不确定度，检验虚拟相位门是否导致IRB衰减超出统计误差。

Result: 在IBM超导处理器上对编译Toffoli门（其中T/T†门通过虚拟Z旋转实现）的案例研究中，跨多个校准运行的Δr在不确定度内与零一致。表明在该编译和执行栈下，虚拟相位的添加或移除不会可测量地改变IRB导出的误差估计。

Conclusion: PA-IRB提供了一种轻量级、抽象感知的诊断工具，用于涉及软件定义相位操作的基准测试工作流。该配对比较方法还可用于对编译门误差中非Clifford组分的贡献施加操作界限，即便这些组件是物理执行而非虚拟实现的。

Abstract: Interleaved randomized benchmarking (IRB) provides a scalable estimate of a gate's error rate, but its standard guarantees require the interleaved gate to be Clifford~\cite{Magesan2012Interleaved,magesan2012characterizing}. In superconducting processors, many non-Clifford phase gates in compiled circuits are implemented virtually as software-defined frame updates rather than as additional control pulses~\cite{mckay2017efficient}. This raises the question of whether inserting or removing such virtual phases measurably changes IRB error estimates.
  We introduce \emph{phase-altered interleaved randomized benchmarking} (PA-IRB), a paired-IRB diagnostic protocol comparing phase-stripped and phase-dressed Clifford interleaving gates derived from the same compiled implementation. PA-IRB reports $Δr=r_d-r_s$ with combined uncertainty to test whether virtual phase gates affect the extracted IRB decay beyond statistical error.
  As a case study, we apply PA-IRB to a compiled Toffoli gate executed on IBM superconducting processors, where the constituent $T/T^\dagger$ gates are implemented as virtual $Z$ rotations. Across tested calibration runs, $Δr$ is consistent with zero within uncertainty, indicating that virtual phase addition or removal does not measurably alter the IRB-derived error estimate under the employed compilation and execution stack. More generally, PA-IRB provides a lightweight, abstraction-aware diagnostic for benchmarking workflows involving software-defined phase operations. The same paired comparison can also be used to place operational bounds on the contribution of non-Clifford components to the compiled gate error, even when those components are physically executed rather than implemented virtually.

</details>


### [119] [Learning the structure of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30358)
*Laura Lewis,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习n量子比特常数局域林德布拉德算符系数的算法，总演化时间O(g d² log(n)/ε²)，实现了哈密顿量学习的最优指标集，并扩展到了开放量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 林德布拉德算符（开放量子系统）的学习比哈密顿量（封闭系统）更具挑战性，存在特有的"混淆"项问题。现有算法无法直接推广，需要设计能处理开放系统的新方法。

Method: 基于简单迭代方法，目标函数由限制在少体区域上的林德布拉德算符的傅里叶系数构成；使用非自适应、无辅助比特的随机泡利测量电路，时间分辨率Θ(1/g)；无需先验知识，依赖平滑的度概念。

Result: 总演化时间复杂度O(g d² log(n)/ε²)；支持准局域和幂律林德布拉德算符的学习；在"混淆"受限时性能提升。

Conclusion: 该算法实现了哈密顿量学习领域的最优指标集，并首次将类似方法扩展到林德布拉德算符；同时导出了结构学习哈密顿量的更简单算法，以及首个从高温吉布斯态学习哈密顿量结构的有效算法。

Abstract: We design an algorithm for learning the coefficients of an $n$-qubit constant-local Lindbladian to $\varepsilon$ error with $O(g d^2 \log(n) / \varepsilon^2)$ total evolution time, where $g$ is the single-site energy and $d$ is the (approximate) degree of the interaction graph. Though Lindbladians present new challenges not present in the special case of Hamiltonians, our algorithm achieves the suite of desiderata attained by state-of-the-art Hamiltonian learning algorithms: (1) it uses non-adaptive, ancilla-free randomized Pauli measurement circuits with a time resolution of only $Θ(1/g)$; (2) it works without knowledge of the structure of the unknown Lindbladian; (3) it depends on a smooth form of degree, thereby supporting the learning of quasi-local and power-law Lindbladians.
  Our algorithm is a simple iterative method, where the objective function consists of Fourier coefficients of the Lindbladian restricted to few-site regions. Its analysis identifies the difficulty unique to open systems, which we call "confusing" terms. For settings where the "confusion" is limited, the performance of the algorithm improves. We demonstrate this for the case of structure learning of Hamiltonians from access to real-time evolution, where we obtain a new algorithm that is significantly simpler than previous work. In addition, using the same iterative method, we design the first efficient algorithm for structure learning Hamiltonians from high-temperature Gibbs states.

</details>


### [120] [Blueprint for a fault-tolerant compound photon-atom quantum architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30385)
*Geva Arwas,Doron Azoury,Daniel Azses,Orel Bechler,Dana Ben Porath,Barak Dayan,David Dentelski,Yaron Jarach,Nadav Kandel,Aviad Landau,Yair Margalit,Alexander Poddubny,Michael Slutsky,Konstantin Yavilberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种光子-原子混合量子计算平台，利用腔QED实现近确定性光子-原子纠缠操作，结合光子的长程连接性与原子的可重复利用性，在RHG格点上实现容错量子计算，光子损失阈值约2.6%每物理门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯光子平台存在纠缠概率低、资源开销大的固有问题；纯原子平台则受限于连通性和可扩展性。需要一种能同时满足可扩展性、连通性和容错纠错要求的混合架构。

Method: 采用腔QED中的对称化Duan-Kimble光子-原子受控相位(CZ)门作为核心原语；以$^{87}$Rb原子耦合光学腔实现态制备、测量、光子产生和纠缠门操作；通过原子重用生成大尺度团簇态；在RHG格点上建立硬件感知噪声模型（非对称损失、光子-原子关联误差）。

Result: 光子损失阈值约2.6%每物理门（每轨迹约15%总损失）；完整Clifford集（Hadamard、相位、CNOT）可横向或折叠横向实现，阈值与恒等通道匹配；提出了两种非Clifford资源态路线（代码隐形传态和魔法态培育）。

Conclusion: 该混合架构结合了光子MBQC的长程连接性与原子资源的近确定性操作优势，在纳秒级时间尺度上实现高保真度门操作，有效降低了容错量子计算的开销，为实用化大规模量子计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires architectures that simultaneously address scalability, connectivity, and error correction under realistic noise constraints. We present a compound photonic-atomic quantum computing platform that uses cavity QED to realize near-deterministic entangling operations between flying photonic qubits and stationary atomic qubits. Photons provide long-range connectivity and scalability via measurement-based quantum computing (MBQC), while atoms supply reusable, near-deterministic resources for photon generation and entanglement, overcoming the inefficiency of purely photonic platforms. The core primitive is a symmetrized Duan-Kimble photon-atom controlled-phase (CZ) gate, robust to experimental imperfections and high-fidelity. Using single $^{87}$Rb atoms coupled to optical cavities, we give protocols for state preparation, measurement, photon generation, and entangling gates on tens-of-nanosecond timescales, and show how large-scale cluster states with effectively unrestricted connectivity and reduced overhead can be generated through atomic reuse. We analyze fault tolerance on the Raussendorf-Harrington-Goyal (RHG) lattice with a hardware-aware noise model capturing asymmetric loss and correlated photonic-atomic errors. Logical memory simulations yield a photon-loss threshold near $2.6\%$ per physical gate ($\sim$15\% total per trajectory). The full Clifford set -- Hadamard, phase, CNOT -- is implementable transversally or fold-transversally at thresholds matching the identity channel, and we propose two non-Clifford resource-state routes (code teleportation and magic state cultivation) within the foliated cluster-state architecture.

</details>


### [121] [Provable Quantum Advantage for Dynamical Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30396)
*Jue Xu,Xiao Yuan,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态量子相变（DQPT）的检测复杂性：证明精确估计DQPT对量子计算机也是难解的；但判定子系统DQPT变体等价于模拟通用量子电路，具有指数级量子优势；并提出一种亚线性时间点数量的量子算法，能以海森堡极限精度搜索局部DQPT临界时间；此外，该方法可推广至经典系统的异常现象检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动态量子相变检测的计算复杂性和成本，以及量子计算能否在该问题上提供优势。

Method: 通过计算复杂性理论证明DQPT估计的难解性；设计量子算法实现局部DQPT临界时间的海森堡极限精度搜索及亚线性时间点缩放；将经典演化编码到量子动力学中实现跨领域加速。

Result: DQPT精确估计是量子计算机也难以处理的问题；子系统DQPT判定具有指数级量子优势；提出的算法在多个时间点上实现海森堡极限精度且亚线性缩放。

Conclusion: 揭示了DQPT检测的复杂性层次，展示了量子计算在相变检测中的优势潜力，并建立了连接经典与量子系统的统一框架。

Abstract: The universal scaling of critical behavior in phase transitions is a cornerstone of physics. Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) are their nonequilibrium analogues: abrupt nonanalyticities that emerge as a quantum system evolves in time. Yet the hardness and cost of detecting this phenomenon remain largely unexplored. We prove that estimating DQPT to a certain precision is intractable even for quantum computers, whereas deciding a subsystem variant of DQPT is as hard as simulating generic quantum circuits, implying a provable exponential quantum advantage. Furthermore, to search for critical times of local DQPTs, we show a quadratically faster quantum algorithm that estimates observables of Hamiltonian dynamics at multiple time points with Heisenberg-limited precision and sublinear scaling in the number of time points. Moreover, through encoding classical evolution into quantum dynamics, our framework enables broader quantum speedups for detecting anomalous phenomena in classical systems.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Computations on Fusion Blanket Molten Salts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30402)
*Susanta Das,Thiago J. Pinheiro Dos Santos,Subhamoy Bhowmik,Milana Bazayeva,Zhen Li,Akhil Shajan,Danil Kaliakin,Fangchun Liang,Vyacheslav S. Bryantsev,Al Geist,Abigail McClain Gomez,Thaddeus Pellegrini,Robert Walkup,Seetharami R. Seelam,Mario Motta,Kenneth M. Merz,,Thomas Beck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将异构量子-经典计算应用于聚变堆FLiBe熔盐中氚结合能的预测，通过嵌入波函数方法将团簇分区，并在IBM量子硬件上用ext-SQD求解最大片段，实现了与全组态相互作用平均0.3 kcal/mol的偏差，同时揭示了片段构建而非片段求解是算法偏差的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLiBe熔盐是聚变堆中氚增殖和回收的关键包层材料，精确预测氚的形态需要准确的电子基态能量，这对传统相关电子结构方法构成挑战，因此需要探索量子-经典混合计算新范式。

Method: 从从头算分子动力学中提取团簇，采用嵌入波函数(EWF)方法将其划分为原子中心片段，最大的片段在IBM量子硬件上通过扩展样本基量子对角化(ext-SQD)求解。

Result: 在九个团簇上，异构量子-经典工作流重现了片段基态能量，与全组态相互作用的平均绝对偏差为0.3 kcal/mol；但分片与非分片构象能量差和氚结合能分别平均偏差12 kcal/mol和110 kcal/mol，表明片段构建是算法偏差的主要来源。

Conclusion: 这是首次在带电离子系统（尤其是无机熔盐）中实现量子-经典混合计算，静电和极化效应使电子关联的精确处理尤为困难，研究结果为未来构建精确可扩展的量子-经典工作流以计算聚变包层中氚形态的自由能估计指明了方向。

Abstract: Molten salts such as FLiBe (2LiF--BeF$_2$) are leading blanket materials for breeding and recovering tritium in fusion reactors. Predicting tritium speciation requires accurate electronic ground-state energies for representative molten-salt clusters, a demanding task for correlated electronic-structure methods. Here we report the first application of heterogeneous quantum--classical computing to tritium binding in FLiBe. Clusters drawn from ab initio molecular dynamics are partitioned by an embedded-wavefunction (EWF) method into atom-centered fragments, and the largest fragments are solved on IBM quantum hardware using extended sample-based quantum diagonalization (ext-SQD). Across nine clusters, the heterogeneous quantum--classical workflow reproduces fragment ground-state energies with agreement to full configuration interaction within 0.7~kcal/mol and a mean absolute deviation of 0.3~kcal/mol. In contrast, fragmented and unfragmented conformational energy differences and tritium binding energies differ by 12~kcal/mol and 110~kcal/mol on average, respectively, identifying fragment construction rather than fragment solution as the dominant source of algorithmic bias. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first such demonstration for a charged ionic system and in particular an inorganic molten salt, where electrostatic and polarization effects make the accurate treatment of electronic correlation particularly challenging. These results also identify areas of future research towards an accurate and scalable quantum--classical workflow to compute free-energy estimates of tritium speciation in fusion blankets.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum-enhanced Monte Carlo Tree Search framework for combinatorial optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30415)
*Yohan Finet,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière,Victor Drouin-Touchette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomTreeSearch，一种将中性原子量子计算机上可原生实现的量子子程序嵌入蒙特卡洛树搜索框架的混合经典-量子算法。在旅行商问题（最多60个城市随机实例和最多100个城市TSPLIB实例）上，该算法表现优于或匹配OR-Tools和模拟退火，且量子子程序相比经典替代子程序能产生更多样化、更高质量的分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算有望更高效地处理优化问题，但Grover算法电路深度超出当前NISQ设备能力，量子退火在实际问题上也难以企及。因此需要一种能在近期量子设备上实际运行的混合算法。

Method: 将中性原子量子计算机上可原生实现的量子子程序（求解最大加权独立集）嵌入蒙特卡洛树搜索框架。在每个扩展步骤中，选择量子处理器提供的候选动作的最大加权独立集，执行这些集体动作以获得子节点。

Result: 在旅行商问题随机实例（最多60城市）和TSPLIB实例（最多100城市）上，混合算法一般匹配或优于OR-Tools和模拟退火。量子子程序相比经典替代子程序产生更多样化且更高质量的分支。

Conclusion: 精心设计的量子子程序嵌入经典搜索框架是近期量子计算在组合优化中实现实用性的有前途路径。

Abstract: Over the past decades, the operations research community has developed numerous effective optimization algorithms, yet quantum computing is emerging as a new computational paradigm with the potential to approach optimization problems more efficiently. Grover's algorithm offers a provable speedup for combinatorial optimization, but its circuit depth places it beyond current noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices. A more accessible alternative is to reformulate the optimization problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and apply quantum annealing; however, practical problem instances remain out of reach for existing hardware. We introduce AtomTreeSearch, a hybrid classical-quantum algorithm that integrates a quantum subroutine natively implementable on neutral-atom quantum computers within a Monte Carlo Tree Search framework. At each expansion step, a maximal weighted independent set of candidate actions provided by the quantum processor is selected, and these collective actions are performed to obtain a child node. We benchmark our method on the Traveling Salesman Problem, with instances of up to 60 cities on random Euclidean instances and up to 100 cities on TSPLIB instances. Our hybrid algorithm generally matches or outperforms both OR-Tools and simulated annealing on these instances, and we find that the quantum subroutine produces more diverse and higher-quality branches compared to classical alternate subroutines. These results suggest that carefully scoped quantum subroutines embedded in classical search frameworks represent a promising path toward near-term quantum utility in combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [124] [Connecting Density Matrix Spectroscopy to Biexciton Entanglement Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30424)
*Yusuke Masaki,Takashi Otaki,Hiroaki Matsueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了密度矩阵光谱（DMS）与双激子态之间的解析关系，并通过数值模拟验证了DMS能够部分捕捉双激子中的纠缠动力学，特别是纠缠熵差 $S_{\mathrm{bi}} - S_k$，从而证明了DMS在研究双激子纠缠动力学中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMS技术已被用于获取双激子发射光子对的时间分辨密度矩阵，但该密度矩阵与双激子态及其纠缠动力学之间的确切关系尚不明确，因此需要建立理论联系并验证DMS的有效性。

Method: 推导了DMS获得的密度矩阵与双激子态之间的解析关系；在二维电子-空穴系统中利用扩展离子Hubbard模型进行数值模拟，对比DMS测量的纠缠动力学与双激子实际纠缠动力学。

Result: DMS能够部分捕捉双激子中的纠缠，特别是能有效反映纠缠熵差 $S_{\mathrm{bi}} - S_k$ 的动力学行为。

Conclusion: DMS技术在获取双激子纠缠动力学信息方面具有有效性，为实验研究双激子纠缠提供了理论和数值支持。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is one of the most intriguing features of quantum mechanics. To investigate the entanglement between two excitons in a biexciton, an experimental technique called density matrix spectroscopy (DMS) has recently been developed. DMS combines stimulated emission tomography and pump-probe techniques to obtain a time-resolved density matrix of the polarization state of a photon pair emitted from the biexciton. The reconstructed density matrix is expected to encode information about the biexciton state and its entanglement dynamics, but the precise nature of this connection has remained unclear. In this paper, we derive an analytical relationship between the density matrix obtained by DMS and the biexciton state. In addition, we perform numerical simulations to compare the entanglement dynamics obtained by DMS with the biexciton's entanglement dynamics in a two-dimensional electron-hole system using an extended ionic Hubbard model. We find that DMS can partially capture the entanglement in the biexciton, in particular, the dynamics of the difference $S_{\mathrm{bi}} - S_k$, where $S_{\mathrm{bi}}$ is the entanglement entropy of the biexciton and $S_k$ is the entanglement in terms of the wavevectors of the excitons that constitute the biexciton. These results demonstrate the validity of DMS for obtaining information about the entanglement dynamics of the biexciton.

</details>


### [125] [Stable Qubit Readout and the Identifiability of Population Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30462)
*Dongdong Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子比特读出中，可重复的响应数据能否唯一确定布居数变化的方向与范围，证明了读出校准方向是关键因素，并给出了精确的解析区间与符号判定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子测量中，稳定的读出统计量常被视为物理响应明确的证据，但稳定性本身并不足以唯一确定是哪个状态量发生了变化。本文旨在分析有限量子比特态集合在二进制读出下，如何从可重复的响应数据中可靠地判定布居数变化的符号或范围。

Method: 文章通过理论分析，研究了完全校准的有限读出族下所有兼容布居数变化的精确闭式区间；构造了相同记录、联合可测的实例，证明相同概率与循环检验下可存在正、零、负多种布居数解释；在仅信任对角读出增益和相干灵敏度的边界时，推导了尖锐的极小极大区间及必要充分符号条件 g>2χ。

Result: 证明了可重复响应数据是否能够认证布居数变化的符号或范围，由校准的测量方向决定而非循环一致性本身；给出了完全校准下的精确闭式区间；提出了符号判定的必要充分条件 g>2χ。

Conclusion: 该研究将实现稳定性与布居数可辨识性区分开来，为量子比特读出校准提供了分析基准，表明仅凭读出稳定性不足以确定物理响应所对应的状态变化。

Abstract: Stable readout statistics are often taken as evidence for a well-defined physical response, but stability alone need not identify which state quantity has changed. We analyze this issue for finite collections of qubit states measured by binary readouts, focusing on changes in computational-basis population. The central question is when reproducible response data certify the sign or range of an underlying population change. We show that the answer is controlled by the calibrated measurement directions, not by loop consistency alone. For a fully calibrated finite readout family, we derive an exact closed-form interval of all compatible population changes. We also construct a same-record, jointly measurable example in which identical probabilities and accepted loop checks admit positive, zero, and negative population interpretations. When only a diagonal readout gain and a bound on coherence sensitivity are trusted, we obtain the sharp minimax interval and the necessary-and-sufficient sign condition $g>2χ$. These results separate implementation stability from population identifiability and provide analytic benchmarks for qubit readout calibration.

</details>


### [126] [Demonstration of unpartible entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30468)
*Philip Held,Laura Ares,Federico Pegoraro,Jonas Lammers,Benjamin Brecht,Jan Sperling,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次实验验证了模式无关纠缠，证明存在一种即使改变模式定义仍能保持的量子纠缠，并通过可重构时间复用干涉仪产生和认证了这种纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠依赖于预定义的子体系（方），当子体系定义改变时纠缠可能消失。为克服这一局限性，实现一种不受模式变换影响的、更鲁棒的纠缠形式，以适应存在噪声和不可信方的实际量子通信场景。

Method: 采用全可重构的时间复用干涉仪，结合测量诱导非线性，产生两个模式上的预示双光子态；利用定制化量子态层析技术进行认证。

Result: 成功生成并验证了在所有正交模式基下均保持纠缠的双光子态，保真度确认了模式无关纠缠的存在。

Conclusion: 模式无关纠缠是一种具有弹性和操作优势的量子关联，在现实量子通信应用中能更好地保障纠缠性能。

Abstract: We report on the first experimental verification of mode-independent entanglement. Commonly, the entanglement of a state is firmly based on pre-defined parties that are correlated, and the state might be disentangled when the definition of the parties is changed. Exceeding this party-dependent concept, we realize a type of quantum entanglement that persists even if the parties, in our case modes, are transformed. This safeguards the performance of entanglement in real-world applications, such as quantum communication settings involving noise and untrusted parties. For the state generation, we present an experimental scheme based on a fully reconfigurable temporally multiplexed interferometer with measurement-induced nonlinearities, which generates heralded two-photon states in two modes that are entangled for all choices of orthonormal mode basis. For the certification process, we utilize a tailored quantum-state tomography, achieving fidelities that validate the presence of mode-independent entanglement as a resilient and operationally advantageous quantum correlation.

</details>


### [127] [Staged Hybridisation for Visual Quantum Reinforcement Learning via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30520)
*Javier Lazaro,Juan-Ignacio Vazquez,Pablo Garcia-Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将知识蒸馏作为视觉量子强化学习的分阶段混合训练策略：先训练经典视觉教师模型并冻结其特征编码器，再蒸馏教师策略行为到紧凑的下游头部（经典或VQC），从而避免端到端训练的困难。实验表明，该方法使浅层VQC头部能在像素级控制任务中习得有效行为，角度编码VQC接近教师水平，幅度编码则更紧凑但更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉环境对量子强化学习极具挑战性——高维观测、RL优化不稳定、变分量子电路训练困难，端到端训练难以收敛。

Method: 分阶段知识蒸馏策略：1) 先训练经典视觉教师模型；2) 冻结教师编码器作为特征接口；3) 将教师策略行为蒸馏到紧凑的下游头部（经典或基于VQC）。VQC头部采用角度编码或幅度编码。

Result: 在CartPole Pixels和Acrobot Pixels上，分阶段KD使浅层VQC头部能获得非平凡的视觉控制行为；角度编码VQC保持接近教师性能；幅度编码VQC更紧凑但更脆弱、对预算敏感、仿真时间长。

Conclusion: 分阶段KD将视觉QRL重新定义为紧凑头部学习问题，为在标准端到端RL循环之外训练小规模量子兼容策略开辟了实用路径。

Abstract: Visual environments are a demanding setting for quantum reinforcement learning (QRL): high-dimensional observations, unstable RL optimisation, and constrained variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are difficult to train jointly. This paper studies knowledge distillation (KD) as a staged hybridisation strategy for visual QRL. Instead of training a hybrid visual agent end-to-end from pixels, we first train a classical visual teacher, freeze its encoder as a feature interface, and distil the teacher's policy behaviour into compact downstream heads. These heads can be classical or VQC-based, enabling small quantum-compatible students to be evaluated under the same frozen representation as compact classical controls.
  We evaluate the pipeline on CartPole Pixels and Acrobot Pixels. The results show that staged KD enables shallow VQC heads to acquire non-trivial visual-control behaviour in settings where direct pixel-based training would be substantially more difficult. Angle-encoded VQC heads retain near-teacher performance, while amplitude-encoded heads push compactness to an extreme regime, at the cost of greater fragility, stronger budget sensitivity, and higher simulation time. Overall, staged KD reframes visual QRL as a compact-head learning problem, opening a practical route for training small quantum-compatible policies outside the standard end-to-end RL loop.

</details>


### [128] [Working with measurement-based computations on qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30525)
*Piotr Mitosek,Miriam Backens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子测量计算中的qudit图态流结构，提出了简化的qudit流定义，证明了聚焦流的规范性，将流查找算法复杂度从O(n⁴)优化至O(n³)，并研究了多种保持流结构的图变换方法，最后提出了生成大规模qudit流计算实例的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的qudit流定义相比qubit流更复杂且难以应用，需要更简洁的定义和更高效的算法来支持基于qudit的测量量子计算研究。

Method: 通过借鉴qubit情形下的成果，简化qudit流定义；证明聚焦流的规范性；改进代数形式化描述以捕获聚焦流；提出O(n³)的流查找算法；研究多种保持流结构的图变换（枢轴操作、顶点删除/插入、流的可逆性）；提出大规模qudit流计算的生成算法。

Result: 给出了更简洁的qudit流定义；证明了聚焦流是规范形式；将流查找算法复杂度从O(n⁴)改进到O(n³)；建立了多种流保持变换；提出了生成大规模qudit流计算实例的方法。

Conclusion: 简化后的qudit流定义和高效算法推动了qudit测量量子计算的理论发展，流保持变换为流结构优化开辟了新路径，生成算法为测试和机器学习提供了数据支持。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computing is a universal model of quantum computation in which successive product measurements of an entangled resource state drive the computation. The non-deterministic nature of measurements necessitates adaptivity to ensure an overall deterministic computation. Flow structures characterise cases in which such an adaptive correction procedure is possible. Recently, flow has been defined in a setting where the resource states are prime-dimensional qudit graph states rather than the usual qubit graph states. Yet, this qudit flow definition is more burdensome to work with than analogous definitions for qubits.
  Here, we give a simpler definition of qudit flow and consider various useful properties of this flow, drawing on results for the qubit case. In particular, we show how to focus qudit flow and argue that focused flow is canonical. We improve the previous algebraic formulation to capture focused flow and use it to obtain an $O(n^3)$ flow-finding algorithm (where $n$ is the number of qudits), matching the best known complexity for qubit flows and improving on the previous $O(n^4)$ result for qudits. Furthermore, we explore multiple flow-preserving transformations, thus opening a pathway to using flow for optimisation. These transformations include pivoting, removal and insertion of certain types of vertices, and reversibility of flow. Lastly, we propose an algorithmic approach to generating large qudit computations with flow, for testing or machine learning.

</details>


### [129] [Bridging the NISQ and Fault-Tolerant Regimes: Generative-ML-Assisted Quantum Selected CI for Molecular Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30551)
*Anurag K. S. V.,Ashish Kumar Patra,Manas Mukherjee,Ruchika Bhat,Sai Shankar P.,Rahul Maitra,Jaiganesh G*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合量子-经典工作流，通过将LCNot-UCCSD ansatz集成到QSCI框架（替代O(N^6) CCSD参数初始化为O(N^4) MP2振幅初始化），并引入基于受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的子空间扩展模型（QSCI-RBM），在Fujitsu FX700模拟器上实现了比现有方法更高效的蛋白质-配体结合能计算，并在包括SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶-卡莫氟复合物在内的多个分子系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质-配体分子系统的结合能计算需要精确处理电子结构，这在经典硬件上呈指数级扩展，而当前量子硬件噪声过大无法支持所需电路深度，现有量子采样对角化工作流存在结构性低效问题。

Method: 1) 将线性缩放CNOT UCCSD（LCNot-UCCSD）ansatz集成到QSCI框架，用O(N^4) MP2振幅初始化替代O(N^6) CCSD参数初始化；2) 引入QSCI-RBM，用受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）作为紧凑生成子空间扩展模型替代SQD框架的构型恢复。在Fujitsu FX700理想态矢量模拟器上使用QARP实现，并通过DMET嵌入处理大型分子。

Result: 在14个受控人工误差水平下对8种不同分子（STO-3G基组）各进行100次独立运行评估，在N₂分子（cc-pVDZ基组）势能面扫描上验证，并成功嵌入DMET处理FDA批准的抗病毒药物金刚烷胺（11个DMET片段）和SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶-卡莫氟复合物（10个片段）。据作者所知，这是LCNot-UCCSD在QSCI中的首次部署，也是首次将DMET-QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM应用于行业相关蛋白质-配体系统。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需当前最先进工作（克利夫兰诊所、RIKEN和IBM Quantum）所需经典计算资源的一小部分，能够实现更高效、更经济的工业药物发现模拟。

Abstract: Calculation of binding energies for protein-ligand molecular systems requires accurate treatment of the electronic structure, a quantum chemistry problem that scales exponentially on classical hardware, while current quantum hardware remains too noisy for the required circuit depths. This report presents a hybrid quantum-classical workflow performed on the Fujitsu FX700 ideal state-vector simulator using QARP that addresses two structural inefficiencies in quantum-sampling-based diagonalization workflows. First, we integrate the Linear Scaling CNOT UCCSD (LCNot-UCCSD) ansatz into the QSCI framework, replacing the $\mathcal{O}(N^6)$ CCSD parameter initialization of the competing LUCJ ansatz approach with $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$ MP2-amplitude initialization. Second, we introduce QSCI-RBM, a variant that replaces the configuration recovery of the SQD framework with a Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM) acting as a compact generative subspace expansion model. Both are evaluated on eight different molecules in STO-3G across 14 controlled artificial error levels with 100 independent runs each, validated on potential energy surface scans of the N$_2$ molecule in cc-pVDZ, and embedded within DMET to treat the FDA-approved antiviral Amantadine (C$_{10}$H$_{17}$N, 11 DMET fragments) and the active region of the SARS-CoV-2 main protease complexed with its covalent inhibitor Carmofur (PDB: 7BUY, C$_{15}$H$_{28}$N$_4$O$_5$S, 10 fragments). To our knowledge, this is the first deployment of LCNot-UCCSD within QSCI on a quantum computing simulator, and the first DMET-QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM application to an industry-relevant protein-ligand system. By utilizing a fraction of the classical computing resources required by the current state-of-the-art work by Cleveland Clinic, RIKEN, and IBM Quantum, this approach enables more efficient and economical drug discovery simulations for the industry.

</details>


### [130] [Revisiting crossed-correlated baths in open quantum systems simulated by HEOM or T-TEDOPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30569)
*Brieuc Le Dé,Etienne Mangaud,Alex W. Chin,Michèle Desouter-Lecomte*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了开放量子系统激发态动力学中的两种计算方法（HEOM和T-TEDOPA）在处理连续温度相关谱密度导致的交叉相关函数时的表现，重点研究了锥形交叉处的集体模式变换，发现T-TEDOPA在频率依赖变换方面优于HEOM，并以对称(苯基乙炔基)苯二聚体的线性振动耦合模型为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析开放量子系统激发态动力学中，当离散线性振动模型被连续温度相关谱密度参数化导致交叉相关函数时，如何高效处理能量间隙集体模式的关联涨落问题。

Method: 采用HEOM和T-TEDOPA两种方法，对涉及两个集体模式的锥形交叉体系进行建模；将初始关联的调谐浴变换为非关联的共享浴以减少计算资源；对比频率依赖变换和频率无关近似变换在两种方法中的适用性。

Result: 完全频率依赖变换对HEOM构成困难，但在特定情况下可导出最优的频率无关近似变换；T-TEDOPA能够高效处理频率依赖变换，但代价是需要管理张量链中的长程耦合。

Conclusion: T-TEDOPA在频率依赖变换问题上优于HEOM，适用于处理由温度相关谱密度引起的交叉相关函数问题；对称(苯基乙炔基)苯二聚体的线性振动耦合模型验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Excited-state dynamics of open quantum systems is analyzed by the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) or the thermalized time-evolving density operator with orthogonal polynomials algorithm (T-TEDOPA) method when a discrete $ab$ $initio$ linear vibronic model is parametrized by continuous temperature-dependent spectral densities leading to crossed correlation functions, i.e. correlated fluctuations of the energy gap collective modes. We focus on a conical intersection involving two collective modes tuning the energy of each excited state and we revisit the transformation of the initial correlated tuning baths to de-correlated shared baths in order to reduce the computational resources. While a completely frequency-dependent transformation poses problems for HEOM, we find that in some particular cases, an optimal approximate frequency-independent transformation may be derived. On the contrary, T-TEDOPA is very efficient and allows to use this frequency-dependent transformation at the price of managing long-range couplings in the tensor chain. An illustrative application is shown by using the linear vibronic coupling model of a planar symmetrical (phenylethynyl)benzene dimer.

</details>


### [131] [Untangling QLDPC Codes with Biased Noise Ancilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30592)
*Runjiang Bi,Kathleen Chang,Shruti Puri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件感知方法，通过使用偏置噪声辅助量子比特（ancilla qubits）来避免量子LDPC码中的钩错误（hook errors）和短循环（short loops），在辅助比特仅受相位翻转错误影响时，可显著提升有效故障距离并减少短循环数量，在辅助比特位翻转错误比相位翻转错误低50倍且电路噪声为2×10^{-3}时，逻辑错误率可降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码在高速率、高距离方面取得了显著进展，但存在两个关键问题：一是难以设计低深度的症候提取电路以避免辅助比特错误传播到多个数据比特（钩错误）；二是基于置信传播的译码器因Tanner图中的短循环而性能受损。本文旨在通过偏置噪声辅助比特来同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 采用硬件感知方法，利用偏置噪声辅助量子比特（仅受相位翻转错误影响），以自行车双变量码（bicycle bivariate codes）和循环超图乘积码（cyclic hypergraph product code）为例，比较辅助比特仅受相位翻转错误与同时受位翻转和相位翻转错误两种情况下的有效故障距离、短循环数量及逻辑错误率。

Result: 当辅助比特仅受相位翻转错误时，传统症候提取电路的有效故障距离显著提高，短循环数量显著降低；在辅助比特位翻转错误比相位翻转错误低50倍、电路噪声为2×10^{-3}的条件下，逻辑错误率改善了近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在无法维持完全偏置的情况下，利用偏置噪声量子比特仍可获得显著且实用的量子纠错优势。

Abstract: Remarkable technical progress has made high-rate, high-distance, quantum low-density parity-check codes (QLDPC) promising candidates for scalable quantum computing. However, it is hard to design low-depth syndrome extraction circuits that do not spread errors from ancilla qubits to multiple data qubits, also known as hook errors, for general QLDPC codes. Additionally, widely used decoders for these codes based on belief propagation are impaired due to short loops in the Tanner graph. Here, we investigate a hardware-aware approach to avoid these hooks and loops using biased noise ancillas. Using examples of bicycle bivariate codes and a cyclic hypergraph product code, which have been widely considered for practical application, we show that the effective fault-distance of the conventional syndrome extraction circuit can be significantly higher and the number of short loops can be significantly lower when the ancillas are subject to phase-flip errors only, compared to when they are also subject to bit-flip errors. This can result in almost an order of magnitude improvement in the logical error rate at circuit noise of $2\times 10^{-3}$ and when bit-flip errors in the ancilla are 50 times less likely than phase-flip errors. Our work demonstrates a significant and practical quantum error correction advantage with biased noise qubits in which full-bias cannot be maintained.

</details>


### [132] [Provably Efficient Learning of Fermionic Correlations under Particle-Number Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30601)
*Yuki Koizumi,Kaito Wada,Toshinori P. Takama,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机轨道旋转的粒子数守恒费米子阴影层析框架，证明了可以同时估计所有k体费米子关联，所需样本量为O_k(η^k/ε²)，与系统大小N无关，并匹配了信息论下界Ω_k(η^k/ε²)，数值实验显示在特定参数下比现有方法减少约一个数量级的查询次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子数对称性对量子态施加了强结构约束，理论上应允许用更少的样本学习局域关联，但此前这种可证明的优势在集体学习局域关联中尚未明确建立。

Method: 开发了基于随机轨道旋转的粒子数守恒费米子阴影层析框架，利用费米子的粒子数对称性设计测量协议，通过随机轨道旋转实现高效采样。

Result: 证明了所有k体费米子关联可以用O_k(η^k/ε²)个样本同时估计，与系统大小N无关；建立了匹配的信息论下界Ω_k(η^k/ε²)；数值实验显示在N=100, η=20, ε=10⁻²下比现有方法减少约一个数量级的查询次数。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了粒子数对称性在费米子可观测量估计中可证明的高效优势，即利用对称性可以最优地降低样本复杂度。

Abstract: Predicting local fermionic correlations is a central task in quantum many-body physics, as these correlations encode many physically relevant local observables. The ubiquitous particle-number symmetry imposes strong structural constraints on quantum states, suggesting that local correlations should be learned with fewer samples than by symmetry-agnostic approaches. However, it has remained unclear whether such a provable advantage exists in collective learning of local correlations. Here, we develop a framework of number-conserving fermionic-shadow tomography based on random orbital rotations. We prove that, for every given order $k$, we can simultaneously estimate {\it all} $k$-body fermionic correlations of an $N$-mode $η$-particle state with a given variance $\varepsilon^2$ using only $O_k(η^k/\varepsilon^2)$ samples, which are independent of the system size $N$. We further establish a matching information-theoretic lower bound $Ω_k(η^k/\varepsilon^2)$ for any adaptive protocol based on single-copy measurements, showing that the $(η^k,\varepsilon)$-dependence is optimal up to constants depending only on $k$. Furthermore, our numerical calculation shows that the proposal reduces the query count by roughly an order of magnitude compared with state-of-the-art methods for one-body correlation estimation in a system of $N=100$, $η=20$ at $\varepsilon=10^{-2}$. This work establishes a provably efficient advantage of particle-number symmetry for fermionic observables estimation.

</details>


### [133] [Repetition-code-based readout error detection and correction across hardware platforms and generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30606)
*Csaba Czabán,Orsolya Kálmán,Sergey N. Filippov,Zoltán Zimborás*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了重复码读出错误检测与纠正在超导（IBM Heron）和离子阱（Quantinuum H1/H2）量子处理器上的表现，发现该技术能提升所有设备的读出保真度，但纠错增益受限于编码引入的门错误，在离子阱平台上效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 读出错误是当前量子处理器的主要噪声源之一，影响期望值估计和基于采样的应用。现有读出错误缓解方法仅校正期望值等聚合量，无法校正单个测量样本。本文旨在通过重复码纠错实现逐样本校正，并跨平台评估其有效性。

Method: 在测量前将每个数据量子比特与辅助量子比特进行冗余编码（重复码），随后通过后选择或多数投票解码。在IBM Heron r1-r3超导处理器和Quantinuum H1/H2离子阱处理器上，独立变化码距、硬件代际和编码布局进行系统实验。

Result: 读出错误检测与纠正在所有测试设备和代际上均提高了读出保真度，即便未编码基线已有显著改进。但额外冗余的价值高度依赖硬件：超导处理器上编码引入的门错误迅速抵消增益，而离子阱处理器因门错误率极低，更大码距仍能保持优势。

Conclusion: 重复码读出纠错是一种跨平台有效的逐样本纠错方法，但其实际收益受硬件门错误率的强烈制约，未来需要在编码开销与纠错能力间根据具体平台进行权衡。

Abstract: Readout errors are one of the dominant sources of noise in current quantum processors, limiting both expectation-value estimation and sampling-based applications. Since they affect only the classical measurement outcomes, they can be addressed using classical coding techniques: immediately before measurement, each data qubit is redundantly encoded with ancilla qubits, and the resulting bit string is decoded either by post-selection or by majority voting. Unlike conventional readout error mitigation, which corrects only aggregate quantities such as expectation values, this approach operates on individual measurement shots and can therefore produce approximately corrected samples. We present a systematic cross-platform and cross-generation experimental evaluation of repetition-code readout error detection and correction. We benchmark the same protocol on IBM Heron r1-r3 superconducting processors and Quantinuum H1 and H2 trapped-ion processors while independently varying the code distance, hardware generation, and encoding layout. We find that both error detection and correction improve readout fidelity on every device and generation tested, even as the unencoded baseline improves substantially across successive hardware releases. At the same time, the value of additional redundancy depends strongly on the underlying hardware. On superconducting processors, the extra gate errors introduced by the encoding rapidly offset its benefits, whereas on trapped-ion processors the much lower gate error rates allow larger code distances to remain advantageous.

</details>


### [134] [Cavity-mediated probabilistic magic $T$-gate injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30628)
*Sofia Cocciaretto,Roberto Menta,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于腔量子电动力学的概率性幻态注入协议，利用受控原子-腔相互作用和条件测量，以每个尝试0.74的成功概率在腔模Fock子空间中制备幻态，并通过隐形传态协议将其注入计算原子，仅需克利福德操作和单个辅助原子即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非克利福德门是实现通用量子计算的必要资源，但其容错实现通常依赖于幻态蒸馏，这会导致量子比特数和电路深度的巨大开销。本文旨在提出一种更高效的幻态制备与注入方案，以降低资源开销。

Method: 利用受控原子-腔相互作用和条件测量，在单腔模的前两个Fock子空间中概率性制备有效幻态（成功概率0.74），随后通过基于缀饰态跃迁的隐形传态协议将腔编码的幻态注入计算原子，整个过程仅需克利福德操作和单个辅助原子进行读出。

Result: 成功概率为每个尝试0.74，且与目标幻态相位无关；所有所需操作（态制备、两量子比特交换门、投影测量）均可通过里德伯原子-腔平台中的现有实验技术实现。

Conclusion: 该方案原则上可扩展至逻辑层操作，通过集体里德伯相互作用和光学非线性实现腔介导的T门直接注入到编码量子比特中，为降低通用量子计算中非克利福德门的资源开销提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Non-Clifford gates are a necessary resource for universal quantum computation, yet their fault-tolerant implementation typically relies on magic-state distillation, which incurs significant overhead in qubit count and circuit depth. In this work, we propose a probabilistic cavity-based magic-state injection protocol. Our scheme exploits controlled atom-cavity interactions and conditional measurements to probabilistically prepare an effective magic state encoded in the first two level Fock subspace of a single cavity mode, achieving a success probability of $0.74$ per attempt, independent of the target magic phase. The cavity-encoded magic state is subsequently injected into a computational atom via a teleportation-based protocol mediated by dressed-state transitions, requiring only Clifford operations and a single auxiliary atom for readout. We show that all required operations -- state preparation, two-qubit exchange gates, and projective measurement -- can be implemented with experimentally available techniques in Rydberg atom-cavity platforms. We further discuss how the scheme can in principle be adapted to operate at the logical level, where collective Rydberg interactions and optical nonlinearities provide a route toward cavity-mediated $T$-gate injection directly into code-encoded qubits.

</details>


### [135] [Provable random-matrix spectral ramp in a static, geometrically local Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30635)
*Matteo Ippoliti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过将双酉电路中的Floquet准能谱嵌入到静态局域哈密顿量的能谱中，首次在有限维度局域希尔伯特空间的静态几何局域多体系统中证明了谱形状因子存在随机矩阵斜坡（线性增长区域）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，量子混沌与随机矩阵统计之间的联系一直是研究热点，但在静态几何局域哈密顿量系统中证明谱形状因子的随机矩阵斜坡行为一直是一个未解决的挑战。此前，Bertini、Kos和Prosen（BKP）仅在双酉Floquet电路中实现了这一里程碑式的结果，而将其推广到静态哈密顿量系统对于理解更广泛类型量子系统的混沌行为至关重要。

Method: 基于BKP的里程碑结果和最近提出的Feynman-Kitaev时钟构造变体，通过将双酉电路的Floquet准能谱嵌入到静态局域哈密顿量的能谱中，并在特定对称性扇区内证明后者的连通谱形状因子继承了BKP斜坡。

Result: 在有限维度局域希尔伯特空间的静态几何局域多体系统中首次证明了谱形状因子存在随机矩阵斜坡。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在时间无关、几何局域的多体系统中验证了随机矩阵斜坡的存在，为静态哈密顿量系统中的量子混沌研究提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: Quantum chaos is commonly associated with the emergence of random-matrix statistics in the spectra of quantum systems. A useful diagnostic is provided by the spectral form factor (SFF), which for random matrix ensembles displays a universal linear-growth regime (`ramp'). In the last decade, a landmark result by Bertini, Kos and Prosen (BKP) identified for the first time a class of geometrically local quantum dynamics of finite-dimensional particles where the SFF provably exhibits a random-matrix ramp: periodically driven (Floquet) qudit chains whose evolution is described by `dual-unitary' circuits. Here, building on the BKP result and on a recently proposed variant of the Feynman-Kitaev clock construction, we obtain a spectral ramp in a class of static, geometrically local Hamiltonians. Our strategy is to embed the Floquet quasienergy spectrum of a dual-unitary circuit into the energy spectrum of a static local Hamiltonian, and to prove that the latter's connected SFF inherits the BKP ramp within a symmetry sector. This is to our knowledge the first proof of a spectral ramp in a time-independent, geometrically local many-body system with finite local Hilbert space dimension.

</details>


### [136] [Authentication in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30636)
*Christopher Battarbee,Suchetana Goswami,Elham Kashefi,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子通信中的认证问题，区分了三种认证类型（经典消息认证、量子消息认证、实体认证），比较了代表性协议的安全假设、设置要求、可组合性和可扩展性，并分析了认证在QKD及其他协议中的应用。核心结论是：认证并非量子网络的固有限制，而是每条协议依赖特定的认证资源，安全性只有在认证资源及其部署假设明确时才有意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信中认证问题常被混淆，缺乏对三种认证功能（经典消息认证、量子消息认证、实体认证）的系统梳理和比较，需要明确不同认证方案的安全假设、设置要求和适用场景。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统调研量子通信中的三类认证协议，从安全假设、设置要求、可组合性、可扩展性四个维度进行比较分析，并通过案例研究（QKD及其他协议）说明认证的实际应用。

Result: 识别出不同认证功能下的代表性协议及其优缺点，提出认证资源概念，明确认证不是量子网络的固有限制，现有文献已提供多种量子安全认证方案。

Conclusion: 认证是协议依赖的资源而非固有限制，安全性声明只有在明确认证资源及其部署假设时才有意义；已有量子安全认证方案可支持不同应用，但需根据功能、假设和安全保证仔细匹配。

Abstract: In this review, we survey the cryptographic task of authentication from the perspective of quantum communication. We review three main flavours of authentication that are often conflated in the literature: authentication of classical messages, authentication of quantum messages, and entity authentication, also covering recent hardware-assisted approaches. We compare representative protocols for each functionality in terms of their security assumptions, set-up requirements, composability, and scalability in large or dynamic networks, and use these criteria to identify and recommend suitable candidates. Finally, applications are surveyed: we provide a detailed case study of authentication and quantum key distribution (QKD), then extend the discussion to protocols beyond QKD, where the role of authentication is more complex. Our take-home message is that an authentication requirement is not an intrinsic limitation of quantum networks: as with all secure communication, each protocol relies on a particular authentication resource, and the security claim of that protocol is meaningful only once the authentication resource and its deployment assumptions are made explicit. At the same time, the existing classical and quantum literature already offers a range of quantum-secure authentication schemes, which can support different applications when carefully matched to the required functionality, assumptions, and security guarantees.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Can AI Draw Science? A Benchmark for Evaluating Scientific Figure Generation by Text-to-Image and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28406)
*Davie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SciDraw-Bench，首个评估科学图表生成质量的基准，包含32个结构化任务和四维评价体系，实验表明领域专用系统在各项指标上显著优于通用文生图模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成基准（如GenEval、T2I-CompBench、DPG-Bench）仅评估自然图像在组合性、物体计数、逼真度等方面的表现，无法衡量科学图表生成所需的关键能力：正确可读的文字标签、实体及其关系的忠实描绘、连贯的图表结构、以及学科绘图惯例的遵守。

Method: 构建了包含32个结构化任务的基准，覆盖8种图表类型和10个学科；每个任务包含自然语言提示和机器可检查的规格说明（包括标签、关系、组件、惯例和负向约束）；设计了四维评估协议：文本保真度（基于OCR的标签召回率和字符错误率）、语义正确性（VLM对照规格说明判断）、结构质量和惯例遵守度，并配套元评估协议和评分者间信度分析。

Result: 在8种图表类型的试点实验中，领域专用系统SciDraw AI在所有维度和图表类型上均显著优于通用基线模型，语义正确性和惯例遵守度的差距最大；文本保真度对所有系统而言仍是最难的维度。

Conclusion: 科学图表生成需要专门的评估基准，领域专用方法在语义正确性和惯例遵守方面优势明显，但文本渲染质量仍是共同瓶颈；代码-图表基线的扩展计划已提上日程。

Abstract: Text-to-image and multimodal generative models are increasingly used to produce scientific figures such as mechanism diagrams, experimental-design schematics, conceptual frameworks, and graphical abstracts. Yet existing image-generation benchmarks (e.g., GenEval, T2I-CompBench, DPG-Bench) evaluate natural images and measure compositionality, object counting, or photorealism. None of them measure what makes a generated scientific figure usable: correct and legible text labels, faithful depiction of entities and their relations, coherent diagrammatic structure, and adherence to disciplinary drawing conventions. We introduce SciDraw-Bench, a benchmark of 32 structured scientific-figure generation tasks spanning eight figure types and ten disciplines, where each task pairs a natural-language prompt with a machine-checkable specification of required labels, relations, components, conventions, and negative constraints. We propose a four-dimensional evaluation protocol: Text Fidelity (OCR-based label recall and character error rate), Semantic Correctness (vision-language-model judging against the specification), Structural Quality, and Convention Adherence, together with a meta-evaluation protocol and a preliminary inter-judge reliability analysis (human-rating validation is ongoing). We evaluate a domain-specific system, SciDraw AI, against representative general-purpose text-to-image models, and outline a code-to-figure baseline as a planned extension. In a pilot over all eight figure types, the domain-specific system substantially outperforms the general-purpose baselines on every dimension and figure type, with the largest gaps on semantic correctness and convention adherence; text fidelity remains the hardest dimension for all systems.

</details>


### [138] [On the Necessity of a Liquid Substrate for Mesh Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28413)
*Hongwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无中心、无共享时钟、无协调器的多智能体网格中，每个智能体在不规则、非同步观测下进行在线状态估计的数学必要条件。证明了两条必要条件：一是需要自适应时间尺度（固定增益滤波器严格次优），二是估计必须依赖观测间隔（无间隔感知网络即使无限深宽也无法恢复）。这两个条件在连续时间液体网络中交汇。LSTM满足第一个，固定连续时间滤波器满足第二个，多时间尺度液体网络同时满足两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无中心、无共享时钟、无协调器的去中心化智能体网格中，每个智能体需要在不可重训的权重上，从不规则、非同步到达的观测中在线折叠其他智能体的投影信息。虽然每个约束单独可解，但同时满足三者下的最优折叠问题尚未解决。作者旨在刻画此类基底必须满足的结构条件。

Method: 从自演化的潜在变量在不规则外生时间被观测的模型出发，理论推导了最优估计器的两个必要条件：1）潜在变量随时间变化，最优估计器需自适应时间尺度，固定增益滤波器严格次优；2）观测到达无时钟，最优估计依赖观测间隔，任何不感知间隔的网络（无论宽度或深度）都无法恢复该依赖。通过合成实验验证了多时间尺度液体网络同时满足两个条件。

Result: 证明了两条必要条件：自适应时间尺度必要性（固定增益滤波器严格次优）和间隔依赖性必要性（与容量无关，规模无法替代缺失的间隔依赖）。LSTM仅满足第一条件，固定连续时间滤波器仅满足第二条件，多时间尺度液体网络同时满足两者。合成实验确认了网络能达到的时间尺度及其分离可精确计算。

Conclusion: 该刻画是必要而非充分的，且仅约束固定权重基底（可重训的网络可通过其他方式达到该类）。每个智能体都受此必要性约束，构成了网格智能的结构性条件。

Abstract: A mesh of sovereign agents has no center: no shared clock, no shared model, and no coordinator to gather data or retrain. Its competence rests on each agent folding the projections its peers emit into a single internal state, online, from observations that arrive at irregular, unscheduled times, on a substrate whose weights it cannot retrain. Any one of these constraints is tractable on its own; folding optimally under all three at once is not. We ask what such a substrate must be, and prove two necessary conditions from one model of a self-evolving latent observed at irregular, exogenous times. Because the latent changes, its optimal estimator is time-varying: an adaptive timescale is necessary, and every fixed-gain filter is strictly suboptimal. And because arrivals are clock-free, the optimal estimate depends on the elapsed gap between them, which no gap-blind network recovers at any width or depth. This second condition is capacity-independent: scale cannot substitute for the missing dependence. The two conditions intersect in the continuous-time liquid class. An LSTM satisfies the first, a fixed continuous-time filter the second, and a multi-timescale liquid network both. Synthetic experiments confirm each: the network attains the timescale, and the separation is computed exactly. The characterization is necessary, not sufficient, and binds fixed-weight substrates: a network free to retrain reaches the class by other means. Proved per agent, the necessity binds every agent of a mesh, a structural condition on mesh intelligence.

</details>


### [139] [Position: RL Researchers Need to Distinguish Between Solving Simulators and Using Simulators as a Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28433)
*Matthew Vandergrift,Esraa Elelimy,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文呼吁强化学习社区明确区分模拟器的两种使用方式：一是以求解模拟器本身为目标，二是将模拟器作为真实部署环境的代理。作者指出两者在约束条件、适用算法和评估指标上存在本质差异，并用实验证明混淆这两种设定会导致误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL研究中，研究者在使用基准模拟器时，往往无意识地将"在模拟器中取得高分数"这一目标异化为"解决模拟器本身"，而忽略了模拟器作为真实环境代理的初衷。这两种目标本质不同，但学界缺乏明确区分，导致研究结论的混淆和误导。

Method: 通过理论分析阐述两种使用方式的差异（约束条件、算法适用性、评估指标），并辅以具体案例和简单实验，展示不区分这两种设定所带来的问题和误导性结论。

Result: 清晰地揭示了求解模拟器与以模拟器为代理两种设定在多个维度的根本区别，并通过实验证据表明混淆二者会导致算法选择失当、评估指标偏差以及研究结论的误导。

Conclusion: 呼吁RL社区在研究中明确声明模拟器的使用方式（求解模拟器 vs. 部署代理），以推动两种设定下各自最佳实验实践的讨论与完善。

Abstract: One goal in reinforcement learning (RL) research is to understand general-purpose sequential decision-making, using benchmark simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment settings. When running experiments, however, the goal of achieving high performance in the simulator can mutate into focusing exclusively on solving the simulator. To achieve high scores, researchers may adopt solutions exclusively meant for solving simulators, rather than learning while the agent is deployed outside a simulator. Solving simulators is also worthy of investigation, but it is a fundamentally different RL research question. In this paper, we argue that RL researchers need to distinguish between two use cases of simulators: solving simulators and using simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment. We first discuss how these two use-cases are importantly different, in terms of constraints on how the agent can use the simulator, which algorithms are appropriate, and which evaluation metrics are appropriate. We then highlight several issues and misleading conclusions that can occur by not making the distinction between these two settings clear, supported with examples and simple experiments. This work is a call to the community to begin clearly distinguishing how they are using simulators in their work, hopefully sparking further discussion on which empirical practices work best in each setting.

</details>


### [140] [Learning to Distributedly Estimate under Partially Known Dynamics: A Covariance-Agnostic Neural Kalman Consensus Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28441)
*George Stamatelis,Kyriakos Stylianopoulos,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协方差无关的神经卡尔曼共识滤波器（CA-NKCF），这是一种在线分布式感知框架，结合了部分领域知识与深度神经网络，在无需噪声统计信息的情况下通过共识权重和卡尔曼式递归更新实现分布式潜状态估计，在线性、混沌（Lorenz）和实际无线跟踪环境中均优于传统分布式卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波和纯无模型深度神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜状态的在线估计是人工智能领域的基本挑战，广泛应用于序列决策、异常检测等领域。现有的分布式卡尔曼和粒子滤波器依赖于噪声统计知识且对模型误设定敏感，而纯无模型深度神经网络又缺乏领域知识的引导，因此需要一种结合二者优势的鲁棒分布式感知框架。

Method: CA-NKCF框架包含三个关键组件：（1）先验估计，（2）优化的共识权重用于信息交换，（3）卡尔曼式的递归更新。该方法不依赖噪声统计知识，融合了部分领域知识与深度神经网络的表征能力，实现去中心化推理。

Result: 在线性系统、混沌Lorenz系统和实际无线跟踪环境中的大量实验表明，CA-NKCF优于传统分布式卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波和纯无模型深度神经网络。其性能优势在噪声水平变化、随机通信拓扑、潜状态维度变化和无线系统散射物体引起的观测杂波密度变化下保持稳定。

Conclusion: CA-NKCF在模型误设定情况下展现鲁棒性，是一种有效的在线分布式潜状态估计方法，融合了模型知识和数据驱动方法的优势。

Abstract: Online latent state estimation constitutes a fundamental challenge within the artificial intelligence field, serving as a foundational tool for diverse applications, including sequential decision making, anomaly and change-point detection. In this paper, a novel online distributed sensing framework, where agents collaborate and exchange information to perform latent state estimation, is presented. The proposed estimator combines available partial domain knowledge with the representation capabilities of deep neural networks. In particular, the designed sensing framework incorporates prior estimates, optimized consensus weights, and Kalman-like recursive updates to perform decentralized inference, without relying on knowledge of noise statistics. Extensive experiments on linear, chaotic (Lorenz), and practical wireless tracking environments reveal that the proposed Covariance-Agnostic Neural Kalman Consensus Filter (CA-NKCF) outperforms traditional distributed Kalman and particle filters as well as purely model-free deep neural networks, exhibiting robustness even when the underlying motion and observation models are misspecified. It is also demonstrated that CA-NKCF's performance advantage remains stable across varying noise levels, random communication topologies, latent state dimensions, and observation clutter densities induced by scattering objects in wireless systems.

</details>


### [141] [S-GAI: Spectral Geometry-Aware Initialization for Sigmoidal MLPs -- From Dataset Geometry to Network Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28444)
*Yi-Shan Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S-GAI，一种基于谱几何感知的初始化框架，利用SVD从图像数据中提取类别特定的谱几何信息（均值、主方向、谱尺度）来初始化单隐层sigmoid MLP的权重，使隐藏层在训练前就编码数据分布的几何结构。实验表明，该方法相比Xavier初始化具有更信息化的初始状态，冻结隐藏层时性能优于随机初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通用逼近定理证明了sigmoid多层感知机的表达能力，但未规定初始权重如何编码数据分布的几何结构。作者希望弥补这一空白，使初始化阶段就能将数据分布的几何信息嵌入网络。

Method: 1) 将sigmoid单元视为光滑半空间门控函数；2) 对每个类别进行SVD分解，得到均值、主方向和谱尺度；3) 通过能量阈值筛选保留的主方向，每个保留方向用两个sigmoid门表示；4) 各类别的门构成共享的隐藏层，直接从训练集初始化；5) 同时构建基于SVD的子空间分类器作为非神经网络的几何参考基准。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10上，S-GAI初始化的MLP初始隐藏状态比Xavier初始化更具信息性，完整训练后达到相当的最终准确率。冻结隐藏层仅训练输出层时，S-GAI显著优于随机门控初始化。

Conclusion: S-GAI有效将类别谱几何嵌入MLP中，为数据分布几何结构与神经网络初始化之间建立了桥梁，在不增加网络容量的前提下提供了更好的训练起点。

Abstract: Classical universal approximation theorems establish the expressive power of sigmoidal multilayer perceptrons, but they do not prescribe how initial weights should encode the geometry of a data distribution. We propose S-GAI, a spectral geometry-aware initialization framework for one-hidden-layer sigmoidal MLPs. Starting from the constructive idea that sigmoid units can act as smooth half-space gates, we move from hand-specified planar geometry to class-wise spectral geometry estimated from image data. For each class, SVD provides a mean, principal directions, and spectral scales. An energy threshold selects the retained directions, and each retained direction is represented by two sigmoid gates. These class-specific gates form a shared hidden layer initialized directly from the training set. We also formulate a SVD-based subspace classifier as a non-neural geometric reference, which tests whether the estimated spectral class geometry is already discriminative before being embedded into the MLP. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and a more challenging CIFAR-10 test show that the S-GAI-initialized MLP starts from a substantially more informative hidden state than Xavier initialization and reaches comparable final accuracy under full training. When the hidden layer is frozen, training only the output layer still gives stronger performance than frozen random gates, providing evidence that S-GAI effectively embeds class-wise spectral geometry into the MLP.

</details>


### [142] [MALOQ: Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28911)
*Manasa Kaniselvan,Alexander Maeder,Denghui Lu,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MALOQ，一个基于SO(2)-等变架构的机器学习框架，用于大规模预测DFT哈密顿量/密度矩阵，支持从几个到10万原子的系统，在Alps超算上实现3000-12000原子的可扩展训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DFT计算代价高昂，难以扩展到大规模体系。ML算子模型可显著降低计算成本，但现有方法在处理大体系、大基组和多种元素时面临挑战。

Method: 基于SO(2)-等变骨干架构，设计自定义数据处理内核处理高秩哈密顿矩阵数据，并采用可扩展的边-并行原子图分布策略，替代传统的分子-并行分布框架。

Result: 在最大分子哈密顿数据集上，每轮训练时间比分子级并行框架减少30%以上；在Alps超算上实现3000-12000原子体系的可扩展训练（192 GPU）和推理（256 GPU）。

Conclusion: MALOQ为大规模电子结构计算提供了一种高效的ML加速方案，支持任意大小的材料图推理，有望将第一性原理量子输运计算推广到此前不可行的尺度。

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) operator models can be trained to predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian/density matrices at significantly reduced computational cost, thus extending electronic-structure calculations to previously unfeasible scales. Here, we introduce MALOQ (Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport), an application built to train on and predict electronic-structure matrices for systems made of few to 100k atoms, described by large basis sets, and covering a wide range of atomic elements. Based on a state-of-the-art, SO(2)-equivariant backbone architecture, MALOQ provides (i) custom data-processing kernels to handle high-rank Hamiltonian matrix data and (ii) a scalable edge-wise distribution of atomic graph(s). Trained on the largest molecular Hamiltonian datasets available today, it reduces time-per-epoch by over 30% compared to a molecule-wise-distributed framework, and enables inference on material graphs of arbitrary size. We demonstrate scalable training and inference for 3,000-12,000 atoms on the Alps supercomputer, up to 192 GPUs and 256 GPUs, respectively.

</details>


### [143] [scKDGM: KAN-guided Dynamic Graph Masked Learning for Single-Cell RNA-seq Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28459)
*Jun Tang,Pengwei Hu,Sicong Gao,Jie Guo,Lun Hu,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scKDGM，一种KAN引导的动态图掩码学习框架，用于scRNA-seq聚类，通过图感知分布保持基因掩码、TAKGCN编码器、动态图构建和跨视图对比学习，在12个真实数据集上优于10个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq聚类面临高维度、稀疏性、dropout和技术噪声导致的表达表示和细胞图构建困难，现有掩码自编码器主要依赖表达恢复进行特征重建，图聚类方法依赖固定KNN图且未将恢复的表达反馈到图优化中。

Method: 提出scKDGM框架，包含：GDP-Mask（图感知分布保持基因掩码）扰动细胞身份；KAN-based TAKGCN编码器学习掩码视图表示；掩码引导的表达恢复构建动态图；跨视图对比学习将恢复信号传递到拓扑更新；ZINB损失建模过离散和零膨胀。

Result: 在12个真实scRNA-seq数据集上，scKDGM在平均NMI和ARI指标上优于10个基线方法。

Conclusion: scKDGM通过动态图掩码学习和跨视图对比学习有效解决了scRNA-seq聚类中表达表示和图优化的耦合问题，显著提升了聚类性能。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) clustering is essential for identifying cell types, but high dimensionality, sparsity, dropout, and technical noise hinder robust expression representation and cell graph construction. Existing masked autoencoders mainly use expression recovery for feature reconstruction, while graph clustering methods usually depend on fixed KNN graphs and do not feed recovered expression back into graph optimization. We propose scKDGM, a KAN-guided dynamic graph masked learning framework for scRNA-seq clustering. scKDGM uses graph-aware distribution preserving gene masking (GDP-Mask) to perturb cell identity, a KAN-based TAKGCN encoder to learn masked-view representations, mask-guided expression recovery to construct a dynamic graph, and cross-view contrastive learning to transfer recovery signals into topology updates. A ZINB loss models overdispersion and zero inflation. Experiments on 12 real scRNA-seq datasets show that scKDGM outperforms 10 baselines in average NMI and ARI.

</details>


### [144] [Counterfactual Residual Data Augmentation for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28460)
*Hossein Mohebbi,Oliver Schulte,Ke Li,Pascal Poupart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CRDA的反事实残差数据增强技术，用于表格回归任务。该方法利用残差不变性生成新的训练样本，在多个数据集上平均降低MLP回归器MSE 22.9%、XGBoost回归器MSE 6.4%，优于现有数据生成和增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界回归任务常面临训练样本有限、采集成本高、观测噪声大等问题，而数据增强在视觉和语言领域效果显著，但在表格回归领域缺乏有效方法。

Method: 提出CRDA，核心思想是回归器建模数据系统成分后，剩余噪声可视为在精心选择特征的小扰动下保持稳定的不变残差，利用这种残差不变性生成新的真实训练样本。方法是模型无关的，适用于各类回归器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CRDA平均降低MLP回归器MSE 22.9%，降低XGBoost回归器MSE 6.4%，在MSE降低方面持续优于现有的最先进数据生成器和增强技术。

Conclusion: CRDA通过向训练数据添加原则性的反事实变化，为噪声多、样本少的回归场景提供了一种简单高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-driven modeling in real-world regression tasks often suffers from limited training samples, high collection costs, and noisy observations. Inspired by the impact of data augmentation in vision and language, we propose a novel Counterfactual Residual Data Augmentation (CRDA) technique for tabular regression. Our key insight is that once a regressor has modeled the systematic component of the data, the remaining noise can be viewed as an invariant residual that remains stable under small perturbations of carefully selected features. We exploit this residual invariance to generate new, yet realistic, training samples, effectively expanding the dataset without requiring additional real data. Our method is model-agnostic and readily applicable to various types of regressors. In experiments across datasets from a variety of benchmark repositories, on average, CRDA reduces an MLP Regressor's MSE by 22.9% and an XGBoost Regressor's MSE by 6.4%. When compared to existing state-of-the-art data generators and augmentation techniques, CRDA consistently outperforms in MSE reduction. By adding principled counterfactual variations to the training data, our method offers a simple and efficient remedy for noise-prone, small-sample regression settings.

</details>


### [145] [ScaleAware-JEPA: Latent Representation for Discovery in Multiscale Physical Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29723)
*Guang-Xing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleAware-JEPA框架，通过约束扩散分解(CDD)将连续标量场分解为像素级尺度分量，并基于尺度坐标定义掩码几何结构，从而在无标签条件下构建稠密的隐空间坐标，实现对复杂物理场多尺度结构的自监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自监督方法在固定图像坐标中定义上下文和目标，其预测任务与连续尺度层级结构组织的物理场不匹配，且有用坐标无法预先获知。

Method: 提出约束扩散分解(CDD)将每个场分解为像素配准的尺度分量，提供定义掩码几何结构的尺度坐标；基于这些尺度坐标构建JEPA目标函数，使上下文覆盖范围与扩散尺度绑定而非固定的patch大小。

Result: 在MHD湍流、星际分子气体和城市夜间灯光结构三个数据集上，学习到的几何结构能映射回连贯的形态学特征，形成无标签、无预定义分割规则的稠密结构图谱。

Conclusion: 通过将隐空间预测与场的尺度层级绑定，ScaleAware-JEPA能在相关结构被预先规定之前构建可用于检查复杂物理模式的隐空间坐标。

Abstract: Continuous physical fields represent a large fraction of data under scientific investigation. Their multiscale structures are central to discovery, yet useful coordinates are not known in advance.
