Standard self-supervised methods define context and targets in fixed image coordinates, posing a predictive task misaligned with fields organized across a continuous scale hierarchy. We introduce ScaleAware-JEPA, a framework that constructs dense, label-free latent coordinates for continuous scalar fields. Constrained Diffusion Decomposition (CDD) separates each field into pixel-registered scale components and provides the scale coordinates that define the masking geometry. The resulting JEPA objective predicts hidden structure with a context footprint tied to the diffusion scale of each component rather than to an arbitrary patch size. Across MHD turbulence, interstellar molecular gas and urban nighttime-light structure, the learned geometry maps back to coherent morphology, forming dense structural atlases without labels or predefined segmentation rules. By tying latent prediction to the scale hierarchy of a field, ScaleAware-JEPA constructs latent coordinates through which complex physical patterns can be inspected before their relevant structures have been prescribed. Code is available at https://github.com/gxli/SA-JEPA.

</details>


### [146] [Singular Learning and Occam's Razor in Deep Monomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28464)
*Kathlén Kohn,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Farhan Shabir,Vahid Shahverdi,Weisheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用多项式代数工具（如Mason定理）研究具有单项式激活函数的深度全连接网络中的临界点，证明在激活函数次数足够大时，临界点精确对应于子网络（即某些神经元不活跃或冗余的参数量构形），为深度神经网络的隐式偏向提供了数学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络优化中梯度动力学受模型架构临界点的影响，这些临界点出现在参数化Jacobian秩亏的位置，是奇异学习理论中研究的最显著奇点。

Method: 使用多项式代数工具（如Mason定理）对具有单项式激活函数的深度全连接网络进行数学分析。

Result: 当激活函数次数足够大时，临界点精确对应于子网络，即某些神经元不活跃或冗余的参数量构形。

Conclusion: 为深度神经网络倾向于收敛到更简单函数的隐式偏向提供了数学视角。

Abstract: In the optimization of neural networks, gradient dynamics are influenced by critical points that arise from the model's architecture. These critical points occur where the Jacobian of the model's parametrization is rank-deficient, and are the most pronounced singularities studied in Singular Learning Theory. We investigate such points in deep fully-connected networks with monomial activations via tools from polynomial algebra such as Mason's Theorem. We show that, for sufficiently large activation degree, criticality occurs precisely at subnetworks, i.e., at parameter configurations where some neurons are inactive or redundant. This offers a mathematical perspective on the implicit bias in deep neural networks, explaining the tendency of these models to converge toward simpler functions.

</details>


### [147] [An Agentic AI Pipeline for Appliance-Level Energy Anomaly Detection and LLM-Driven Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28467)
*Dihia Falouz,Aida Douaibia,Amine Bechar,Youssef Elmir,Abbes Amira,Adel Oulefki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度时序预测、变分异常检测和LLM推理的端到端智能体管线，用于办公楼设备级能源监测，生成优先级排序的可操作维护建议。动态检索在保持性能的同时将上下文来源从6个减少到3-6个，最佳后端得分90.4/100，本地7B模型通过全部16个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有办公楼设备级能源监测系统产生过多噪声告警，非专业设施管理人员难以有效利用这些告警进行维护决策。

Method: 提出端到端智能体管线：(1) 混合SSA-LSTM预测模型进行30分钟级能耗预测；(2) 每设备LSTM-VAE注意力变分自编码器检测异常日能耗；(3) 三级LangChain管线：Context Agent动态检索RAG源（3个核心+最多3个条件性源，上限8推理步），Diagnosis Agent生成结构化JSON诊断，Report Agent生成可读报告；(4) 反射性记忆层整合操作员反馈。

Result: 动态检索与全量静态检索在所有后端上性能匹配，同时将平均上下文从6个降至3-6个来源/事件。最佳后端得分90.4/100，70分阈值通过率100%，完全本地化的7B参数模型通过全部16个场景（持续/瞬态尖峰、意外关机、系统性事件）。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体管线通过动态检索机制在保证推理质量的同时显著降低LLM上下文开销，且轻量级本地模型即可达到全量场景覆盖，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Appliance-level energy monitoring in office buildings produces noisy alerts that non-expert facility managers struggle to use. This paper proposes an end-to-end agentic pipeline that combines deep time-series forecasting, variational anomaly detection, and LLM-based reasoning to generate prioritized, actionable maintenance recommendations. The system tracks seven office appliances using a hybrid Singular Spectrum Analysis (SSA) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) forecasting model, and applies a per-appliance LSTM Variational Autoencoder (VAE) with attention to flag abnormal daily consumption episodes. A three-stage LangChain pipeline begins with a Context Agent that always retrieves three core RAG sources (model reliability, hourly baseline, and expert knowledge) and conditionally adds up to three more (forecast context, anomaly history, global baseline) based on event characteristics, capped at eight reasoning steps. A Diagnosis Agent converts the evidence into a structured JSON diagnosis, and a Report Agent renders a human-readable narrative. A reflective memory layer incorporates operator feedback. The dashboard shows real-time 30-minute forecasts, intraday consumption, the previous day anomaly report, and a feedback form. We evaluate the forecasting model, anomaly detector with appliance-specific thresholds, and LLM reasoning on a 16-scenario benchmark including sustained and transient spikes, unexpected shutdowns, and systemic events, comparing five LLM backends under static vs. dynamic retrieval. Dynamic retrieval matches full static retrieval across all backends while cutting average context from six to three-six sources per event. The best backend scores 90.4/100 with a 100% pass rate at a 70-point threshold, and a fully local 7B-parameter model passes all 16 scenarios.

</details>


### [148] [Modelling Emotional Memory in Children with Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28470)
*Henry Groves,Lucia F. Jackson,Barbara-Anne Robertson,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究展示了情绪效价如何影响儿童识别记忆的顺序依赖结构：儿童对一系列情绪效价玩具的正确回忆不仅取决于当前玩具的效价，还取决于其前后玩具的效价。经典张量网络模型引入效价后达到77.98%的准确率，远超标准心理模型，证明了量子启发方法在建模顺序依赖现象（如情绪记忆）中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准心理模型虽然确认了事件中的顺序依赖差异，但准确率低，且未能反映情绪对象如何影响集合中其他对象的记忆。因此需要更有效的模型来捕捉这种情绪记忆的顺序依赖结构。

Method: 采用经典张量网络模型，引入情绪效价因子，对儿童识别一系列情绪效价玩具的顺序记忆进行建模分析。

Result: 经典张量网络模型在建模儿童情绪记忆顺序依赖现象中达到77.98%的准确率，而标准心理模型准确率较低。

Conclusion: 尽管该模型并非严格的"量子认知"模型，但准确率的大幅提升证明了量子启发方法在建模顺序依赖现象（如情绪记忆）中的价值。同时，该研究引入的任务协议为使用经典和类量子认知模型探索儿童情绪时间记忆提供了新颖的现实工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate how emotional valence influences the order-dependent structure of children's recognition memory: correct recall of a sequence of emotionally-valenced toys depended not just on the valence of a given toy itself, but also on the valence of the toys shown before and after it. Whilst standard psychological models confirm that order-dependence differs across an event (a set of toys shown in sequence), accuracy is low and the model does not reflect how memory for an emotional object influences others in the set. A classical tensor network model factoring in valence is able to achieve a 77.98\% accuracy in modelling the results of the study. While not strictly a ``quantum cognition'' model, this massive increase in accuracy shows the value of quantum-inspired methods for modelling order-dependent phenomena, such as emotional memory. Further, the task protocol we introduce presents a novel, real-world tool for exploring emotional temporal memory in children for analysis using classical and quantum-like models of cognition.

</details>


### [149] [A Trainable-by-Parts Operator Learning Framework: Bridging DeepONet and Karhunen-Loeve Expansions for Large-Scale Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28519)
*Christian Munoz,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL-DNN的可扩展算子学习框架，用于大规模PDE问题（尤其是地质碳封存）。该模型在170万网格单元、50时间步的3D数据集上训练，无需下采样即可实现全分辨率预测，压力RMSE为1.1psi（相对误差0.04%），CO2饱和度RMSE为0.0146（相对误差5%），训练仅需20分钟（单GPU），相比DeepONet误差降低19%-7%且加速两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模PDE问题（如地下流动、气候建模、地质碳封存）存在维度灾难、内存限制和训练数据有限等挑战，现有算子学习方法难以高效处理高分辨率大规模问题。

Method: 基于KL-DNN框架，利用静态属性的低秩SVD构建潜在空间，采用嵌套Karhunen-Loeve展开处理动态压力场，实现无需子采样或空间粗化的全分辨率预测。

Result: 在含170万网格单元和50时间步的100个GCS模拟样本上，压力RMSE为1.1psi（0.04%相对误差），CO2饱和度RMSE为0.0146（5%相对误差）；单GPU训练20分钟，推理时间不到1分钟；相比DeepONet误差降低19%（压力）和7%（饱和度），速度提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提KL-DNN框架作为大规模PDE问题的实用且准确的解决方案，可实现快速不确定性量化、历史拟合和实时决策支持。

Abstract: Training operator-learning models for large-scale problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) is challenging due to the curse of dimensionality, memory constraints, and limited training data. These challenges arise in many scientific and engineering applications, including subsurface flow, climate modeling, and geological carbon storage (GCS). In this work, we propose a scalable operator-learning framework based on the Karhunen-Loeve Deep Neural Network (KL-DNN) and demonstrate its performance for modeling GCS. The model is trained on a dataset comprising 100 samples of large-scale simulations in a three-dimensional domain with 1.7 million cells and 50 time steps. The KL-DNN method constructs latent spaces using low-rank singular value decomposition of static properties and a nested Karhunen-Loeve expansion for dynamic pressure fields, enabling full-resolution predictions without subsampling or spatial coarsening. The KL-DNN model achieves an average root mean square error (RMSE) of 1.1 psi for pressure (0.04% relative error with respect to the average pressure in the domain) and RMSE of 0.0146 for CO2 saturation (5% relative error with respect to the average saturation inside the plume). The model requires 20 minutes of training on a single GPU, representing a 19% reduction in the pressure errors, 7% reduction in the saturation error, and a two-order-of-magnitude speedup compared to DeepONet trained on the same dataset. These results, along with inference time of less than one minute, establish the proposed model as a practical and accurate solution for large-scale PDE problems, enabling rapid uncertainty quantification, history matching, and real-time decision support.

</details>


### [150] [A Gravitational Interpretation of Fine-Tuning Reversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28525)
*Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“微调回归的引力解释”框架，认为大模型早期训练形成主导行为流形，后续对齐/微调仅产生浅层偏移，因此再次微调时会沿历史定义的回归方向(v_rev)发生回退。实验表明v_rev方向与早期回归高度相关，阻断该方向可显著降低有害输出率（从19.0%降至8.5%），验证了该方向是早期对齐后回归的因果中介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现微调无害数据会部分逆转早期训练行为，安全性能在良性后对齐更新下退化，已消除能力会重新出现，潜在特征可通过看似无关的监督发生迁移。这些现象缺乏统一解释框架，本文旨在通过训练历史的几何视角提供系统性理解。

Method: 提出引力解释假设：早期大训练阶段创建主导行为流形，后期对齐/专业化阶段产生浅层位移，后续微调继承指向主导流形见证者的持久回归分量(v_rev)。通过测量表示漂移沿v_rev方向的分量变化，并采用选择性阻断运动沿v_rev方向的方法验证因果相关性。

Result: 对齐与v_rev的余弦相似度从第一次更新后的0.429±0.052上升到第20步的0.647±0.021；24个运行-步数对中每个观测到的对齐都超过各向同性激活空间零假设的p99。阻断v_re方向运动后最终对齐从0.648±0.009变为-0.211±0.021，有害输出率从19.0%±4.0%降至8.5%±1.5%，且任务成本很小。

Conclusion: v_rev是早期对齐后回归的因果相关中介方向，该框架有助于解释多种后对齐脆弱性现象。但研究不声称v_rev是唯一安全方向，也不声称直接观察到主导流形，而是识别出一个稳健的、由历史定义的方向来解释和部分控制早期回归动态。

Abstract: Fine-tuning on harmless data can partially undo behaviors acquired earlier in training. Safety can erode under benign post-alignment updates, unlearned capabilities can re-emerge, latent traits can transfer through apparently unrelated supervision, and related post-alignment fragility appears in other generative settings. We argue these phenomena are usefully viewed through a common training-history lens. Our hypothesis is geometric: large early training phases create dominant behavioral manifolds, while later alignment or specialization phases are shallower displacements from them. Subsequent fine-tuning can therefore inherit a persistent reversion component pointing back toward a witness of the dominant manifold. We call this the gravitational interpretation of fine-tuning reversion. Across our main settings, representational drift rapidly acquires a component along a history-defined reversion direction (v_rev). In our main track, alignment with v_rev rises from cos = 0.429 +/- 0.052 after the first update to 0.647 +/- 0.021 by step 20. Across 24 run-step pairs, every observed alignment exceeds the p99 of an isotropic activation-space null. We demonstrate that selectively blocking motion along v_rev changes the final alignment at T=100 from 0.648 +/- 0.009 to -0.211 +/- 0.021 and reduces harmfulness from 19.0% +/- 4.0% to 8.5% +/- 1.5% with little task cost. These results support v_rev as a causally relevant mediator of early post-alignment reversion in our setup. Importantly, we do not claim that v_rev is the unique safety direction, nor that the dominant manifold is directly observed; rather, we identify a robust, history-defined direction that explains and partially controls early reversion dynamics.

</details>


### [151] [NIVA: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Actionable Earth System Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28546)
*Anisha Pal,Aodhan Sweeney,Kyle Heyblom,Kalai Ramea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NIVA，一个面向地球系统多模态耦合动力学的AI基础模型，通过在海洋和大气双模态设置上的概念验证，展示了其学习跨模态物理结构的能力，为次季节到季节预测奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动气象模型虽提升了预报技能并降低了计算成本，但无法建模地球系统各圈层之间的耦合动力学，这限制了将可预测性扩展到约2周以上时间尺度的能力。

Method: 提出NIVA多模态基础模型，在大规模地球系统模拟数据上训练，学习大气、海洋、冰、陆地等各圈层的统一表征；本文以海洋和大气双模态设置作为受控概念验证，评估基础模型学习耦合动力学的能力。

Result: NIVA学习了物理上有意义的跨模态结构，并通过准确预测主要气候指数，捕获了气候变异的关键模态。

Conclusion: 为基础模型能够学习地球系统耦合动力学提供了初步验证，为次季节到季节（S2S）预测奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI-driven weather and climate modeling have improved forecast skill while reducing computational cost. However, existing data-driven approaches are limited in their ability to model coupled Earth system dynamics, which is required for extending predictability beyond the ~2-week horizon. To address this, we introduce NIVA, a multimodal foundation model designed to learn unified representations across Earth system components. While the full framework targets atmosphere, ocean, ice, and land interactions, we focus here on a two-modality setting (ocean and atmosphere) as a controlled proof of concept to evaluate whether foundation models can learn coupled dynamics. Trained on large-scale Earth system simulations, NIVA learns physically meaningful cross-modal structure, providing a foundation for subseasonal-to-seasonal prediction. As initial validation, we show that NIVA captures key modes of climate variability through accurate prediction of major climate indices.

</details>


### [152] [Improving Coherence in Hierarchical Time Series Forecasting using Structured Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28553)
*Ruchi Pakhle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化时序融合模型（HTF），通过将结构化层次嵌入与一致性感知损失函数集成到Temporal Fusion Transformer中，在训练阶段直接嵌入一致性约束，同时提升层次化时间序列预测的准确性和一致性，在M5沃尔玛和能源消耗数据集上优于传统调和方法和深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层次化预测方法（如Bottom-Up和MinT）通过后处理强制一致性，但无法建模复杂的非线性时序依赖和协变量交互，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 在Temporal Fusion Transformer基础上引入结构化层次嵌入，并设计一致性损失函数，在训练过程中惩罚子节点聚合预测与父节点预测之间的差异，使模型同时学习时序动态和结构一致性。

Result: 在M5沃尔玛预测数据集和层次化能源消耗数据集上，HTF在降低预测不一致性方面效果显著，同时预测精度优于经典调和方法和深度学习基线。

Conclusion: 将一致性约束嵌入训练目标而非作为后处理步骤，可以有效提升层次化时间序列预测的质量；注意力可视化和嵌入分析进一步揭示了时序与结构信息在层次预测中的作用机制。

Abstract: In many real-world applications, such as retail sales, energy usage, and supply chain planning, forecasting is performed across hierarchical structures. These structures often represent aggregations (e.g., products to categories to regions), where forecasts must not only be accurate but also coherent, meaning that lower-level predictions sum correctly to higher-level forecasts. Traditional statistical methods, such as Bottom-Up and MinT, enforce coherence through post-processing but fail to model complex nonlinear temporal dependencies and covariate interactions.
  We propose Hierarchical Temporal Fusion (HTF), a novel extension of the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) that integrates structured hierarchical embeddings with a coherence-aware loss function to ensure consistent forecasts across all levels of a hierarchy. Rather than applying reconciliation after forecasting, HTF embeds coherence directly into the training objective. The coherence loss penalizes the difference between aggregated child forecasts and their corresponding parent forecasts during training, enabling the model to learn both temporal dynamics and structural consistency simultaneously.
  We evaluate HTF on two publicly available benchmark datasets: the M5 Walmart forecasting dataset and a publicly available hierarchical energy consumption dataset. Results demonstrate that HTF substantially reduces forecast incoherence while improving forecasting accuracy compared with classical reconciliation methods and deep learning baselines. In addition, attention visualization and embedding analysis provide insight into how temporal and structural information contribute to hierarchical forecasting performance.

</details>


### [153] [Geometric Measurements of the Axiom of Choice in Neural Proof Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28572)
*Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用 Lean 4 内核层面的公理依赖追踪，将 471,260 个 Mathlib 声明按对选择公理的传递依赖进行划分，并用 42,355 个追踪定理的策略调用序列训练自监督证明编码器。研究发现，经典证明在选择公理依赖图中距公理越近，异常分数、重构损失和密度超水平包含度三个互补度量表现出系统性下降（浅层边界 AUC 0.847），在距离 9+ 后与构造证明不可区分。操作上，aesop 策略对构造定理的解决率是经典定理的 13 倍，神经混合方法将差距缩小至 5 倍，几何异常分数可预测 aesop 失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将选择公理这一百年哲学争论转化为可测量的几何和操作层面的实证问题，揭示经典证明与构造证明之间可量化的结构差异，并探索其对神经定理证明器的实际影响。

Method: 使用 Lean 4 内核追踪 471,260 个 Mathlib 声明对选择公理的依赖，过滤出 42,355 个定理的策略调用序列；训练自监督证明编码器，测量异常分数、重构损失和密度超水平包含度三个几何指标；通过长度、文件、作者、主题等对照实验进行鲁棒性检验；用 aesop 策略和神经混合 ReProver 进行操作性评估。

Result: 经典证明在依赖图中距公理越近几何指标系统性地下降，浅层边界 AUC 0.847，距离 9+ 后与构造证明不可区分；aesop 解决构造定理的速度是经典定理的 13 倍，神经混合方法将差距压缩至 5 倍；几何异常分数可独立于证明长度预测 aesop 失败。

Conclusion: 选择公理在证明空间中具有可测量的几何关联，该关联服从单参数混合律，且对神经定理证明器具有操作层面的可测后果，为经典与构造证明的长期哲学区分提供了实证基础。

Abstract: The axiom of choice has divided the foundations of mathematics for over a century, but the distinction between classical and constructive proofs has remained a philosophical and methodological one. We use Lean 4's kernel-level tracking of axiom dependence to show that the axiom of choice has a measurable geometric correlate in proof space that obeys a one-parameter mixture law and has operational consequences for neural theorem provers. To do this, we partition $471{,}260$ declarations of Mathlib by transitive dependence on the axiom of choice and represent a filtered population of $42{,}355$ traced theorems by their sequences of tactic invocations. We use the constructive proofs in this dataset to train a self-supervised proof encoder and show that when using it to measure classical proofs, three complementary measurements (anomaly score, reconstruction loss, and density-superlevel containment) exhibit a common decline with the proof's distance from the axiom in the dependency graph, from sharp separation at the shallow boundary (AUC $0.847$ at distance $2$) to indistinguishability at distance~$9{+}$. Robustness controls show that the signature survives length, file, author, and topic controls, and replicates under full-source encoders trained on normalised proof source. Operationally, we show that on an evaluation sample of $251$ Mathlib theorems, Lean's \texttt{aesop} tactic solves constructive theorems at $13\times$ the rate of classical ones, and a neural-guided hybrid using the ReProver tactic generator compresses the gap to $5\times$. The geometric anomaly score predicts \texttt{aesop} failure beyond proof length, providing an operational link between the geometric signature and prover performance.

</details>


### [154] [Replica Symmetry Breaking and Algorithmic Thresholds in Empirical Risk Minimization under Multi-Index Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28573)
*Andrea Montanari,Kangjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维非凸经验风险最小化问题，提出了增量近似消息传递（IAMP）算法，并在高维渐近框架下精确刻画了该算法在监督学习设置中达到的训练误差以及训练与测试误差的关系。数据由标准高斯特征向量和依赖于未知k维子空间投影的响应变量组成，模型依赖于数据的m维投影（如m神经元神经网络）。研究揭示了多项式时间算法在经验风险景观中可达的部分，并预期IAMP在多项式时间算法中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型训练涉及高维非凸优化，尽管损失函数存在大量局部最优点，基于梯度的优化方法却常收敛到接近全局最优。作者希望精确理解多项式时间算法在高维非凸经验风险景观中能够达到什么样的解，即哪些部分是计算可及的。

Method: 提出增量近似消息传递（IAMP）算法，在高维渐近（n,d→∞, n/d→α）框架下分析其性能。数据生成：特征向量x_i为标准高斯，响应y_i依赖于x_i在未知k维子空间上的投影。模型通过m维数据投影进行经验风险最小化（如m神经元神经网络）。

Result: 精确刻画了IAMP算法在高维渐近下达到的训练误差，以及训练误差与测试误差之间的关系。基于相关模型的已有研究，预期该算法在多项式时间算法中达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 在高维监督学习设定下，IAMP算法能够有效优化非凸经验风险函数，其性能很可能是多项式时间算法中的最优。这为理解高维非凸优化的计算可及性提供了精确的理论刻画。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models are trained by optimizing high-dimensional non-convex empirical risk functions. Such cost functions can have a multitude of local optima and yet, gradient-based optimization appears to converge to near-global optima.
  Within a simple supervised learning setting, we develop a precise picture of which parts of the empirical risk landscape are accessible by polynomial-time algorithms. We are given i.i.d. pairs $\{(\boldsymbol{x}_i,y_i):\; 1 \le i\le n\}$ with $\boldsymbol{x}_i\in \mathbb{R}^d$ standard Gaussian feature vectors, and $y_i\in\mathbb{R}$ response variables that depend on $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ through their projections on an unknown $k$-dimensional subspace. We use empirical risk minimization to learn a model that depends on an $m$-dimensional projection of the data (e.g., an $m$-neurons neural network).
  We propose an incremental approximate message passing (IAMP) algorithm and precisely characterize the training error it achieves, as well as the relation between test and training error, in the high dimensional asymptotics $n,d\to\infty$, with $n/d\toα\in (0, +\infty)$. Based on earlier work in related models, we expect that the performance achieved by our algorithm is optimal among polynomial-time algorithms.

</details>


### [155] [What LLMs explain is not what they believe: Evaluating explanation sufficiency under models' own input beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28615)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并正式定义了自由文本解释的充分性概念，通过自洽充分性（SCSuff）这一信息论指标评估LLM解释是否包含足够信息来解释其输出生成过程。实验表明LLM解释普遍不充分，且SCSuff可从内部表征中预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高风险领域的部署日益增多，链式思维和后验解释等自由文本被用于证明模型输出合理性，但现有方法无法确定这些解释是否包含足够信息来解释模型的输出生成过程，缺乏对解释充分性的形式化评估标准。

Method: 将经典充分性从特征归因推广到任意解释，证明解释充分性依赖于输入分布；利用LLM自身基于解释生成替代输入；提出自洽充分性目标并引入信息论指标SCSuff，无需预定义偏见或捷径即可评估自由文本解释。

Result: SCSuff与针对性扰动测试结果一致；解释充分性随输入分布变化；LLM解释普遍不充分，且与模型大小、准确率或输出熵弱相关；可从最终token隐藏状态预测SCSuff高分与低分。

Conclusion: SCSuff可指导检测和改进充分的LLM解释，为自由文本解释的可靠性评估提供了形式化框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, where free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought and post-hoc rationales are used to justify model outputs. Yet it remains unclear whether these explanations are sufficient, i.e., if they contain enough information to explain the model's output-generating process. We generalize classical sufficiency from feature attributions to arbitrary explanations and prove that explanation sufficiency can change depending on the input distribution, which must be explicitly defined for LLM explanations. We propose using the LLM itself to generate alternative inputs conditioned on an explanation, capturing its beliefs about possible inputs. We formalize self-consistent sufficiency as a goal for free-text explanations and introduce an information-theoretic metric, SCSuff, that enables evaluation of free-text explanations without relying on predefined biases or shortcuts. Our experiments show that SCSuff agrees with targeted perturbation tests where applicable and demonstrate that explanation sufficiency can vary with the input distribution. We find LLM explanations are generally insufficient and weakly correlated with model size, accuracy, or output entropy. Analysis of final-token hidden states shows that top and bottom SCSuff scores can be predicted from internal representations, suggesting that SCSuff can guide detection and improvement of sufficient LLM explanations. The code for this paper is available at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/self-consistent-sufficiency .

</details>


### [156] [Randomized Exploration for Linear Bandits via Absolute Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28616)
*Toshinori Kitamura,Shuai Liu,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出绝对汤普森采样（ATS）及其集成变体EATS，通过用绝对值替换符号探索噪声确保期望乐观性，在保持TS计算效率的同时实现了UCB风格的简洁遗憾分析，在线性bandit中达到$\tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$的遗憾界，并在理论和实践上桥接了随机探索与确定性乐观主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCB算法遗憾分析简洁但计算成本高，TS计算高效但因其非乐观性质导致分析困难（需要复杂的反集中论证）。作者希望设计一种兼具TS计算效率和UCB分析简洁性的算法。

Method: 提出ATS（Absolute Thompson Sampling），将TS中的符号探索噪声替换为绝对值，确保期望乐观性。进一步提出EATS（Ensemble Absolute Thompson Sampling），取多个绝对值扰动的最大值并用集成大小归一化，随集成规模增大趋近于UCB目标。

Result: ATS达到$\tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$遗憾界，匹配现有线性bandit中TS的界。EATS在中等集成大小时即展现出强性能。

Conclusion: ATS和EATS在理论和实践上桥接了随机探索（TS）与确定性乐观主义（UCB），提供了一种既计算高效又分析简洁的探索方法。

Abstract: In stochastic linear bandits, the canonical Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) algorithm admits a simple frequentist regret analysis but can be computationally demanding, while Thompson Sampling (TS) is computationally attractive yet typically harder to analyze due to its non-optimistic nature. We propose Absolute Thompson Sampling (ATS), a simple modification of TS that ensures optimism in expectation by replacing the signed exploration noise with its absolute value. This preserves the computational efficiency of TS while avoiding the technically involved anti-concentration arguments common in TS analyses, enabling a simple UCB-style regret analysis. We show that ATS achieves $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$ regret, matching existing bounds for TS in linear bandits. We further introduce Ensemble Absolute Thompson Sampling (EATS), which takes the maximum over multiple absolute perturbations with normalization by the ensemble size. As the ensemble size grows, EATS converges to the UCB objective, recovering UCB behavior in the limit. Experiments show that moderate ensemble sizes already yield strong performance. Our results point to a bridge between randomized exploration and deterministic optimism both in theory and practice.

</details>


### [157] [Improving Patient Subtyping on Longitudinal Data using Representations from Mamba-based Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28623)
*Md Mozaharul Mottalib,Rahmatollah Beheshti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督Mamba的模型，用于从电子健康记录（EHR）数据中学习有效的患者表示，从而改进患者的子类型划分（聚类/分组）。在公共和私有真实EHR数据集上，该模型在预测任务上优于基线模型，并且通过聚类技术展示了其在基于时序记录的患者子类型划分中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用EHR数据进行患者子类型划分对精准医疗具有重要意义，但EHR数据固有的复杂性和不规则性使得时序子类型划分任务极具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种自监督的Mamba模型，用于学习有效的EHR表示；结合聚类技术对患者进行子类型划分；在公开和私有真实EHR数据集上评估。

Result: 所提出的模型设计在预测任务上优于竞争性基线模型；通过多种聚类技术的评估，验证了模型在基于时序EHR记录的患者子类型划分中的有效性。

Conclusion: 自监督Mamba模型能够有效学习EHR表征，从而提升患者子类型划分的效果，为精准医疗提供有价值的见解。

Abstract: Effective sub-typing (also known as grouping or clustering) of patients using their electronic health record (EHR) data can greatly inform precision medicine efforts. However, subtyping temporal EHR datasets is known to be challenging due to inherent EHR issues, including complexity and irregularity. In this study, we propose a self-supervised Mamba-based model that learns effective EHR representations and enables enhanced patient subtyping. We evaluate the proposed model on public and private real-world EHR datasets to classify the data based on the available labels and subtype patients based on the representations learned from the model. Through an extensive set of experiments, we demonstrate that our model's design choices lead to better performance compared to competitive baseline models for prediction. Moreover, we evaluate several clustering techniques to demonstrate that our findings offer valuable insights into subtyping patients based on temporal records from EHR models\footnote{Our implementations are available at https://github.com/healthylaife/triplet_mamba.

</details>


### [158] [When More Sampling Hurts: The Modal Ceiling and Correlation Ceiling of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28661)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在测试时通过多次采样（test-time scaling）来提高覆盖度（至少一次正确答案的概率），但实际部署时必须选择一个最终答案，而选择能力存在上限。随着采样次数增加，模型可能更加确信一个错误答案，导致覆盖度上升但选择性能停滞，形成“可识别性差距”。本文提出“有效样本数”这一指标来判断何时停止采样，避免过度采样带来的计算浪费和性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理系统通过增加采样次数（test-time scaling）来提升覆盖度，但这忽略了实际部署中需要选择单一答案的瓶颈。覆盖度上升并不等同于选择性能提升，过度采样不仅浪费计算资源，还可能使模型更确信错误答案。因此需要明确何时停止采样。

Method: 本文识别了两个关键上限：模态上限（modal ceiling，投票结果在几十次采样后饱和）和相关性上限（correlation ceiling，基准测试评分更早饱和）。提出“有效样本数”（effective number of samples）作为统一指标，用于在采样过程中动态判断何时已达到收益天花板。

Result: 覆盖度随采样次数单调上升，但选择性能在少量采样后即达到饱和；过度采样会导致模型对错误答案的置信度增加；投票在几十次采样内收敛，基准评分收敛更快。有效样本数可揭示当前采样是否已越过收益递减点。

Conclusion: 推理系统的瓶颈不在于生成更多候选答案，而在于识别正确答案。采样应适可而止——答案的识别能力限制了选择性能的上限，超出此限度的采样徒增成本而无收益，甚至可能降低最终答案质量。

Abstract: People overthink; language models over-sample, and the extra effort can talk both into a worse answer. Reasoning systems answer a hard question by sampling it many times (test-time scaling), and the more they draw, the more often a correct answer turns up somewhere, so coverage, the fraction of problems with at least one correct try, climbs and appears to be progress. But a deployed system must return one answer, and choosing it, not knowing which try is right, is selection; selection is capped, and past a point extra samples only make the model surer of a confident mistake, even as every draw adds cost. The gap between climbing coverage and stalled selection, the identifiability gap, is the answer a model can produce but not pick. So the real question is not whether to sample but how far, and the answer is: not far. For picking an answer, the vote has already settled within a few dozen draws, the modal ceiling; for scoring a benchmark, sooner still, the correlation ceiling. Beyond that, extra draws cost compute and add nothing, and can even make the answer worse. This paper turns the cutoff into a single number, the effective number of samples, that any sampling run already reveals. The bottleneck is recognizing a right answer, not generating one.

</details>


### [159] [Closed-Form Steepest Descent Direction toward Flat Minima: Reducing Upper Bounds on the Loss Hessian Eigenspectrum in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28662)
*Yuto Omae,Kazuki Sakai,Yohei Kakimoto,Makoto Sasaki,Yusuke Sakai,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对三隐藏层神经网络的交叉熵损失，解析推导了Hessian最大特征值的Wolkowicz-Styan上界梯度，并提出HSR正则化方法，沿该梯度最速下降方向更新参数，有效收敛到平坦极小点，避免尖锐极小和鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦性假说认为损失景观的平坦度与神经网络泛化能力相关，但现有方法大多关注过程设计，难以从理论上刻画数据分布和网络结构如何决定通向平坦极小值的方向，且解析推导该方向通常不可行。

Method: 解析推导了三层神经网络交叉熵损失Hessian的Wolkowicz-Styan上界的梯度，并据此提出Hessian Spectral Range (HSR) Regularization，沿该梯度的最速下降方向更新参数。

Result: HSR正则化能够收窄Hessian特征值谱，避免尖锐极小点和鞍点，促进收敛到平坦极小点。

Conclusion: 这是首个报道无需数值近似即可促进收敛到平坦极小点的闭式梯度的工作，其理论分析有望推动神经网络的进一步发展。目前该方法仅适用于交叉熵损失和三隐藏层架构。

Abstract: The flatness hypothesis suggests that flatness of the loss landscape, as measured by the eigenvalues of the loss Hessian, correlates with better neural network generalization. While various algorithms reduce these eigenvalues, most focus on procedural design, leaving it unclear how data distributions and NN parameters structurally determine directions toward flat minima. Characterizing these directions analytically is generally intractable. To overcome this mathematical difficulty, recent studies derived the Wolkowicz-Styan (WS) upper bound on the maximum eigenvalue of the cross-entropy loss Hessian in three-layer NNs. Although this upper bound is differentiable, its gradient was not derived. Therefore, we analytically derive the gradient of the WS upper bound to characterize directions leading to flat minima. Based on this, we propose Hessian Spectral Range (HSR) Regularization, which updates parameters along the steepest descent direction of the WS bound. Experiments demonstrate that HSR Regularization narrows the Hessian eigenvalue spectrum, avoids sharp minima and saddle points, and promotes convergence to flat minima. Although the applicability of this method is currently limited to cross-entropy loss and three-layer architectures, to the best of the authors' knowledge, this is the first study to report a closed-form gradient that promotes convergence to flat minima without numerical approximations. Therefore, the theoretical analysis of this gradient is expected to contribute to the further development of NNs.

</details>


### [160] [Entropy Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Stochastic Differential Games in a Regime-Switching Jump-Diffusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28669)
*Congde Hu,Zhuo Jin,Danping Li,Lin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机微分博弈中参数误设和环境突变问题，提出一种分布控制方法，将最优策略表征为基于连续状态、离散状态和参数的动作概率分布，构建了熵正则化零和随机微分博弈的强化学习框架，并推导了HJBI方程、半解析解及Actor-Critic算法，最后通过投资博弈实例验证了温度参数和状态转换的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机微分博弈框架存在参数误设定（parameter misspecification）和结构性环境突变问题，需要更鲁棒的控制方法。

Method: 1) 引入分布控制方法，将最优策略表征为动作上的概率分布；2) 建立熵正则化零和随机微分博弈强化学习框架（ERRL-ZSSDGs），应用于状态切换跳扩散过程；3) 利用动态规划原理推导耦合HJBI方程组，通过值函数梯度表达均衡策略；4) 对线性二次问题，求解耦合常微分方程组获得半解析解；5) 对一般问题，开发Actor-Critic策略改进算法近似值函数和均衡策略。

Result: 在投资博弈数值示例中，验证了温度参数（熵正则化强度）和状态切换对最优策略和值函数的影响。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效应对了参数不确定性及环境突变问题，为随机微分博弈提供了分布鲁棒的控制框架。

Abstract: To address parameter misspecification and sudden structural environmental changes in conventional stochastic differential game (SDG) frameworks, this paper introduces a distributional control approach that characterizes optimal strategies as probability distributions over actions, conditioned on the continuous state, the discrete regime state, and parameters. This forms a reinforcement learning framework for entropy-regularized zero-sum stochastic differential games (ERRL-ZSSDGs) in a regime-switching jump-diffusion process. Using the dynamic programming principle (DPP), we derive the associated coupled systems of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) equations, from which equilibrium strategies are expressed via gradients of the value function. For linear-quadratic problems, semi-analytical solutions for both value function and equilibrium strategies are obtained by solving a system of coupled ordinary differential equations (ODEs). In more general settings, an Actor-Critic policy improvement algorithm is developed to approximate the value functions and equilibrium policies across different regimes. The method is applied to an investment game, and numerical examples illustrate the effect of the temperature parameter and regime transitions on optimal policies and values.

</details>


### [161] [Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Linear-Quadratic Stackelberg Differential Games in Regime-Switching Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28671)
*Congde Hu,Danping Li,Lin Xu,Wenying Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵正则化强化学习的方法来解决连续时间扩散框架下马尔可夫体制切换的线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈问题，通过推导弱耦合HJBI方程、集成神经网络逼近价值函数以及新型采样技术，有效处理高维系统并避免次优均衡陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态规划和HJBI方法在求解SDGs时计算复杂度高，尤其在高维系统中面临困难，且经典方法容易陷入次优均衡，缺乏有效的探索机制。

Method: 提出熵正则化强化学习（ERRL）方法；推导带有熵正则化的探索性弱耦合HJBI方程；集成神经网络以逼近依赖于体制的价值函数并高效求解高维PDEs；设计新型采样技术以提升计算可处理性。

Result: 数值结果表明，与常规方法相比，该框架通过探索性策略有效逃离次优陷阱，在层次决策问题中取得更优效果。

Conclusion: 熵正则化和神经网络逼近在应对环境突变下的层次决策问题中发挥了关键作用，为实现鲁棒解提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Stackelberg differential games (SDGs) provide a powerful framework for hierarchical decision-making in stochastic and continuous-time environments, yet their solution remains computationally challenging due to the complexity of traditional dynamic programming and Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) methods, especially in high-dimensional systems. This paper proposes an entropy-regularized reinforcement learning (ERRL) approach for linear-quadratic SDGs (LQ-SDGs) within a continuous-time diffusion framework governed by Markovian regime switching. The key innovation lies in deriving exploratory weakly-coupled HJBI equations with entropy regularization, which promotes stochastic policies that actively avoid suboptimal equilibria -- a limitation of classical SDG methods. Neural networks are integrated to approximate regime-dependent value functions and solve high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) efficiently, while a novel sampling technique enhances computational tractability. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework compared to conventional approaches, particularly in escaping suboptimal traps through exploratory policies. The study highlights the critical role of entropy regularization and neural network approximations in achieving robust solutions for hierarchical decision-making problems under abrupt environmental shifts.

</details>


### [162] [Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28674)
*Michael Cardei,Jose M Munoz,Oscar Barrera,Shreyas K Chandrahas,Partha Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTDF 是一种将采样时可行性操作与混合类型表格扩散模型相结合的金融数据生成方法，能在零违规条件下生成符合监管和经济约束的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域的生成模型需要同时满足数据真实性和严格的监管与经济目标约束，而标准表格扩散模型无法满足这些要求。

Method: CTDF 在反向扩散采样循环中引入无需训练的可行性操作符（feasibility operator），以强制执行硬约束，支持模拟、法律合规和外推等应用。

Result: 在大规模金融数据集上的实验表明，CTDF 实现了零约束违规，并提升了稀缺数据的效用。

Conclusion: CTDF 为生成可信且合规的金融合成数据提供了稳健方法，为金融领域的严格生成建模与分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Generative models in finance face the dual challenge of producing realistic data while satisfying strict regulatory and economic objectives, a requirement that standard tabular diffusion models cannot provide. To address this difficulty, we introduce Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance (CTDF), a novel integration of sampling-time feasibility operations with mixed-type tabular diffusion in financial applications. By incorporating a training-free feasibility operator into the reverse-diffusion sampling loop, CTDF enforces hard constraints for applications such as simulation, legal compliance, and extrapolation. Extensive experiments on large-scale financial datasets demonstrate zero constraint violations and improvement in scarce data utility. CTDF establishes a robust method for generating trustworthy and compliant synthetic data, opening new avenues for rigorous generative modeling and analysis in the financial domain.

</details>


### [163] [A Path-Space Formulation of Prediction in World Models: From a Single Action to Prediction, Planning, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28751)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AI世界模型预测的路径空间框架，将世界模型视为未来轨迹的概率测度，而非单步条件分布的序列。在有效马尔可夫潜动力学下，路径测度呈现Onsager-Machlup形式。通过将潜动力学分解为可逆与不可逆分量，从模型展开中引入熵产生的操作度量。在小规模注意力模型中，注意力不对称性随数据不可逆性训练而习得；对称化注意力会抑制熵产生并选择性削弱不可逆动力学的长程预测。结果表明不可逆性可作为预测世界模型的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI世界模型的预测方法多基于单步条件分布序列，缺乏对轨迹整体概率分布的建模，难以统一处理预测、规划和不确定性评估。作者提出路径空间视角，旨在将预测、规划和不确定性统一为单一作用泛函上的操作。

Method: 1) 将世界模型定义为未来轨迹的概率测度；2) 在马尔可夫潜动力学假设下，推导出Onsager-Machlup形式的路径测度；3) 将潜动力学分解为可逆与不可逆分量；4) 从模型rollouts中引入熵产生的操作度量；5) 在小规模注意力模型上验证注意力不对称性与数据不可逆性的关系；6) 通过对称化注意力检验其对熵产生和预测性能的影响。

Result: 注意力不对称性在训练中习得，且与数据的不可逆性成比例。对称化注意力会抑制熵产生，同时选择性地削弱不可逆动力学的长程预测能力，但保留松弛性预测性能。

Conclusion: 不可逆性可作为预测世界模型的计算资源。预测的基本对象应是未来路径的分布而非状态的分布，路径空间框架为理解世界模型中的预测、规划和不确定性提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We propose a path-space formulation of prediction in AI world models. Rather than sequences of one-step conditional distributions, we argue that a world model implicitly defines a probability measure over future trajectories. In the local regime where latent dynamics admit an effective Markovian description, this path measure takes the Onsager-Machlup form. Within this framework, prediction (most probable trajectory), planning (constrained optimization), and uncertainty (fluctuations) emerge as operations on a single action functional. We decompose the latent dynamics into reversible and irreversible components and introduce operational measures of entropy production from model rollouts. In controlled small-scale attention-based models, we find that attention asymmetry is acquired during training in proportion to the irreversibility of the data. Symmetrizing the learned attention suppresses entropy production and selectively degrades long-horizon prediction of irreversible dynamics while preserving relaxational prediction. These results suggest that irreversibility may serve as a computational resource for predictive world models. More generally, the fundamental predictive object is a distribution over future paths rather than states.

</details>


### [164] [Hierarchical Decision Making with Structured Policies: A Principled Design via Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28764)
*Yuexuan Wang,Jingyuan Zhou,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习-最优控制（RL-OC）框架，通过逆向优化从专家演示中学习底层优化问题的目标函数，使底层策略与长期任务目标对齐，在资源分配和碰撞避免任务中优于端到端RL、学习增强OC和现有分层RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层策略存在两个关键局限：(i) 基于RL的方法难以保证严格约束满足；(ii) 基于OC的方法往往依赖短视且计算代价高昂的公式。尽管分层RL-OC架构试图调和这些权衡，但底层优化问题的公式化仍然未被充分探索，常依赖启发式或短视的目标。

Method: 提出一种将上层目标抽象与结构化底层决策制定系统性集成的原则性框架。采用逆向优化方法，从专家演示中推断底层问题的目标结构，确保底层策略的目标与整体长期任务目标保持一致。

Result: 在网络资源分配和连续碰撞避免两类决策任务上评估，结果表明该方法在效率和决策质量上一致优于端到端RL、学习增强OC以及现有分层RL方法等强基线。

Conclusion: 通过逆向优化将专家知识注入底层优化问题的目标结构，能够有效对齐分层框架中上下层目标，实现更优的决策性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical decision-making frameworks are pivotal for addressing complex control tasks, enabling agents to decompose intricate problems into manageable subgoals. Despite their promise, existing hierarchical policies face critical limitations: (i) reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods struggle to guarantee strict constraint satisfaction, and (ii) optimal control (OC)-based approaches often rely on myopic and computationally prohibitive formulations. To reconcile these trade-offs, hierarchical RL-OC architectures have emerged as a promising paradigm. However, the formulation of the lower-level optimization within these frameworks remains underexplored, often relying on heuristic or myopic objectives. In this work, we propose a principled framework that systematically integrates upper-level goal abstraction with structured lower-level decision making. We adopt an inverse optimization approach to inform the structure of the lower-level problem from expert demonstrations, ensuring that the objective of the lower-level policy remains aligned with the overall long-term task goal. To validate the approach, our framework is evaluated on distinct decision making tasks: network-based resource allocation and continuous collision avoidance. Empirical results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines based on end-to-end RL, learning-augmented optimal control, and existing hierarchical RL approaches in both efficiency and decision quality.

</details>


### [165] [Generative Learning as a Tool to Improve Perception of Emotional Body Motion Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28769)
*Huakun Liu,Miao Cheng,Xin Wei,Felix Dollack,Victor Schneider,Hideaki Uchiyama,Chia-huei Tseng,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Monica Perusquia-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Transformer的生成模型是否能够从日本演员的动作捕捉数据中隐式学习情感身体运动表达，无需显式情感-运动指导。模型以13种离散情感标签为条件生成动作，机器识别准确率22.80%，人类（日本评分者）识别准确率24.91%。此外，该生成模型在增强情感识别模型、提取代表性情感运动模式以及合成情感强度间平滑过渡等实际任务中展示了效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感身体运动是非语言交流的关键要素，在虚拟现实化身和社会机器人等技术中有效传达这些表达至关重要。然而，由于情感线索的微妙性、个体差异和文化差异，生成准确的情感表达表征极具挑战性。作者希望探究生成模型能否直接从文化背景下的动作捕捉数据中隐式学习情感身体运动，无需显式的情感-运动指导。

Method: 使用49名日本演员的情感表演数据集，训练了一个基于Transformer的生成模型，以13种离散情感标签为条件生成 expressive motions。评估从两方面进行：(1) LSTM分类器进行机器识别评估；(2) 日本评分者进行人类感知研究评估。此外还评估了生成模型在三个实际任务中的效用：增强情感识别模型、提取代表性情感运动模式、合成情感强度间的平滑过渡。

Result: 机器识别准确率为22.80%，人类识别准确率为24.91%。生成模型在情感识别增强、代表性运动模式提取和情感强度过渡合成等任务中展示了实际效用。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了隐式、数据驱动的生成建模在增强情感计算应用和加深对情感表达理解方面的潜力。

Abstract: Emotional body motion expressions are an essential element of non-verbal communication. Effectively conveying these expressions through technology is of utmost importance, for example, with virtual reality avatars and in social robotics. Recent advances in generative models have opened new opportunities for advancing research on emotional body motion learning. However, generating accurate emotional expression representations is challenging, given the subtlety of emotional cues, individual variability, and cultural differences. We investigate whether a generative model can implicitly learn emotional body motions directly from culturally grounded motion-capture data, without explicit emotion-motion guidance. Using a dataset of emotional performances by 49 Japanese actors, we trained a Transformer-based generative model to generate expressive motions conditioned on 13 discrete emotion labels. We evaluate the generated motions from two perspectives: (1) an LSTM-based classifier to assess recognizability by machine observers, achieving a recognition accuracy of 22.80%, and (2) a human perception study with Japanese raters to assess alignment with human affective interpretations, yielding a recognition accuracy of 24.91%. Beyond these, we evaluate the utility of generative modeling for three practical tasks: augmenting emotion recognition models, extracting representative emotion-specific motion patterns, and synthesizing smooth transitions between emotion intensities. Our findings highlight the potential of implicit, data-driven generative modeling to enhance affective computing applications and our understanding of emotion expressions.

</details>


### [166] [On design-unbiased algorithmic Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28795)
*Li-Chun Zhang,Siu-Ming Tam,Luis Sanguiao-Sande,Wesley Yung,Anders Holmberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无真实数据模型情况下，基于已知概率抽样设计，如何在有限总体中实现机器学习算法的无偏预测与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习算法（如kNN、随机森林）追求预测性能（最小化MSE或最大化F-score），但在官方统计等场景中，无偏性至关重要。本文旨在探索除真实数据模型外，哪些条件能使算法预测达到无偏。

Method: 基于已知的样本概率设计（而非假设分布或模型），研究训练数据从总体中的抽样方式、算法调优方法以及样本外预测性能的无偏评估。

Result: 提出了在有限总体中实现无偏预测/分类的条件，包括抽样设计、算法调优策略及无偏性能评估方法。

Conclusion: 通过已知概率设计而非假设模型，可以在无真实数据模型的情况下实现算法预测的无偏性，这为官方统计等领域的ML应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, such as k-Nearest Neighbours (kNN) or random forest, eschew the ideal of true data models in favour of predictive performance. However, minimising the MSE or F-score cannot lead to unbiasedness directly, which is important in many situations such as official statistics. We study the conditions of algorithmic ML, other than the existence and knowledge of true data models, which lead to unbiased prediction or classification for a given finite population, including how the training data may be sampled from the population, how a trained prediction algorithm can be tuned to achieve unbiased prediction or classification for that population, and how the performance of out-of-sample prediction or classification can be assessed unbiasedly. The inference is based on the known probability design of samples and training sets, rather than any assumed distributions or models.

</details>


### [167] [HARD-KV: Head-Adaptive Regularization for Decoding-time KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28831)
*Yuxuan Yang,Feiyang Ren,Bowen Zeng,Dalin Zhang,Jinpeng Chen,Gang Chen,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARD-KV通过级联缓存层次结构和对数几率校准机制，解决了长上下文LLM推理中自适应压缩算法与静态推理引擎之间的"静态-动态"不匹配问题，在10k+ token场景下实现高达2倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理面临根本性冲突：头部自适应压缩算法（如Top-p核采样）通过动态波动内存预算提供更优精度，但现代推理引擎（如vLLM）要求刚性静态内存模式以利用CUDA Graphs和PagedAttention。

Method: 提出HARD-KV统一框架，包含：1) Cascade Cache层次结构，在密集、稀疏和压缩三级管理token生命周期；2) Logits Calibration机制，将多样化重要性指标归一化到统一概率空间，实现跨异构头的一致Top-p预算分配；3) 系统级方案，将碎片化动态索引重写为连续物理布局，兼容高性能推理引擎。

Result: 在数学推理基准（AIME, U-Math）上，HARD-KV相比静态基线的吞吐量提升高达2倍，并在10k+ token场景下保持高保真生成。

Conclusion: HARD-KV有效弥合了动态选择与刚性系统约束之间的差距，为长上下文LLM推理提供了一种兼顾精度与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference faces a fundamental conflict: head-adaptive compression algorithms (e.g., Top-$p$ nucleus sampling) offer superior accuracy by dynamically fluctuating memory budgets, yet modern inference engines (e.g., vLLM) demand rigid, static memory patterns to leverage CUDA Graphs and PagedAttention. We resolve this ``Static-Dynamic'' mismatch with HARD-KV, a unified framework that that bridges dynamic selection with rigid system constraints. HARD-KV introduces a Cascade Cache hierarchy, managing the token lifecycle across dense, sparse, and condensed tiers. Crucially, we propose a Logits Calibration mechanism that normalizes diverse importance metrics into a unified probability space, enabling consistent Top-$p$ budgeting across heterogeneous heads. To bridge the efficiency gap, we offer a system-level solution, which rewrites fragmented, dynamic indices into contiguous physical layouts compatible with high-performance inference engine. Extensive experiments on math-reasoning benchmarks (AIME, U-Math) verify that HARD-KV achieves up to 2$\times$ throughput improvement over static baselines while maintaining high-fidelity generation in 10k+ token scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/SuDIS-ZJU/HARDInfer.

</details>


### [168] [Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition for Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28833)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向量子高斯过程回归的非均匀预算分配策略，通过三种闭式敏感性度量（$|α_iα_j|$、留一残差、边际似然梯度）指导Neyman最小方差分配，并引入均匀覆盖下限防止噪声过集中。在四个UCI基准和两组合成实验上，该方法在中预算区间比均匀分配提升了10-21%的测试RMSE，且有效性延伸到真实量子核（ZZ/Pauli-Z）、四种下游任务（贝叶斯求积、异方差回归、超参数学习、多输出协同克里金）。但在UCI特征嵌入ZZ核时增益消失，归因于指数浓度区域中分配策略无发挥空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的量子核分类研究已表明非均匀分配可以提高预算效率，但高斯过程回归的下游量（全谱后验方差、对数行列式、边际似然）与核误差的耦合比分类更紧密。现有的分类导向分配方法无法直接适用于回归场景，因此需要专门针对GP回归推导敏感性度量并设计分配规则。

Method: 推导了三种闭式配对级敏感性：$|α_iα_j|$（预测方差敏感性）、留一残差（预测误差敏感性）、边际似然梯度（超参数学习敏感性）。基于Neyman最小方差分配公式结合这些敏感性分配采样预算；同时为防御噪声估计下的灾难性过集中，添加了由Frobenius下界保证的均匀覆盖下限。

Result: 在四个UCI基准和两个合成RBF+伯努利受控研究中，非均匀分配在中预算区间比均匀分配提升10-21%测试RMSE。在真实ZZ和Pauli-Z量子核的量子自然数据上，低预算区间增益13-15%（p<0.05配对检验）。该增益可迁移到贝叶斯求积、异方差回归、超参数学习、多输出协同克里金等四个下游任务。但在UCI特征嵌入ZZ核时增益消失。

Conclusion: 非均匀shot分配策略能够显著提升量子高斯过程回归在中等预算下的性能，且适用于多种量子核和下游任务；其增益来源于利用任务敏感性进行精准预算分配，但在数据处于指数浓度区域时受限。

Abstract: Quantum kernel estimation on near-term hardware is shot-budgeted: every entry of the kernel Gram matrix is a Bernoulli expectation that must be sampled with a finite number of circuit executions. Recent work on quantum kernel classification has shown that allocating shots non-uniformly across kernel entries, weighted by their downstream task sensitivity, can reduce the shot budget required to reach a target accuracy. We extend this idea to Gaussian process (GP) regression, a setting whose downstream quantities (full-spectrum posterior variance, log-determinant, marginal likelihood) couple to kernel error more tightly than the sign-only outputs of classification. We derive three closed-form pair-level sensitivities predictive coupling $|α_iα_j|$, leave-one-out residual, and marginal-likelihood gradient and plug them into a Neyman-style minimum-variance allocation rule. To prevent catastrophic over-concentration when the warm-up sensitivity estimate is itself noisy, we add a high uniform coverage floor justified by a Frobenius lower bound on the missing-entry perturbation. On four UCI benchmarks and two synthetic RBF + Bernoulli controlled studies, the resulting allocator delivers $10$--$21\%$ test-RMSE improvement over uniform allocation across the moderate-budget regime. The gain transfers (i) to genuine ZZ and Pauli-Z quantum kernels on quantum-natural data ($-13$--$15\%$ at low budget, $p<0.05$ paired) and (ii) to four downstream tasks (Bayesian quadrature, heteroscedastic regression, hyperparameter learning, multi-output Cokriging). On UCI features embedded into a ZZ kernel the gain disappears, consistent with the exponential-concentration regime where shot allocation has nothing to exploit.

</details>


### [169] [Fisher-Routed Mixture of Experts for Federated Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28835)
*Wenhao Yuan,Chenchen Lin,Jian Chen,Jinfeng Xu,Zewei Liu,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFMX框架，通过Fisher路由的混合专家模型解决联邦学习中的类增量学习挑战，包括容量冲突、灾难性遗忘、Non-IID数据异构性和类别对齐问题，实现了自适应专家专业化，并取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习扩展到类增量学习场景面临三大挑战：1)共享模型过载导致的容量冲突和灾难性遗忘；2)Non-IID数据带来的异构性；3)同步类别错位问题。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出FedFMX框架，包含三个核心模块：1) Fisher路由专家评分(FRES)模块，通过基于Fisher的稳定性成本和基于梯度的可塑性增益估计专家重要性；2) 自适应专家选择(AES)模块，通过量化边际贡献自适应确定专家子集；3) 路由感知正则化(RAR)，实现负载均衡和高效联邦训练。

Result: 理论证明了O(T^{-1})的收敛速率；在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FedFMX相比现有SOTA方法具有优越性。

Conclusion: FedFMX通过自适应专家专业化和Fisher路由机制，有效解决了联邦类增量学习中的容量冲突、灾难性遗忘和数据异构性等核心挑战，为FL在增量学习场景下的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) emerged as a promising distributed machine learning paradigm. However, extending FL to the class incremental learning scenarios introduces unique challenges: 1) Capacity conflict and catastrophic forgetting from the shared model overloading, 2) Heterogeneity from Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (Non-IID) data, and 3) Synchronized class misalignment. In this paper, we propose \textbf{F}isher-Routed \textbf{M}i\textbf{X}ture of Experts for \textbf{Fed}erated Class-Incremental Learning (\textsc{FedFMX}), a novel framework to address these challenges via adaptive expert specialization across clients. The crucial insight is to route each sample to an expert subset that jointly optimizes knowledge acquisition and retention. Specifically, we introduce a Fisher-Routed Expert Scoring (FRES) module to estimate expert importance via Fisher-based stability cost and gradient-based plasticity gain. Then, we design an Adaptive Expert Selection (AES) module by quantifying marginal contributions for adaptive expert subset determination. Finally, by the routing-aware regularization (RAR), we achieve load balance and efficient FL training. We theoretically prove the $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$ convergence rate. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art methods demonstrate the superiority of \textsc{FedFMX}.

</details>


### [170] [The Contagion Tensor: A Framework for Measuring Output-Distribution Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems -- and Auditing the Claims It Enables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28839)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Contagion Tensor测量框架和CAF指标，用于量化LLM输出分布在模态、智能体和时间步上的耦合效应。通过消融实验和真实API验证，发现图像条件可产生超线性耦合（CAF=1.40→0.87消融后），而文本条件无此效应。GPT-4o-mini上视觉模态CAF达1.72，验证了仿真预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统中，输出分布之间的耦合效应缺乏定量化、可证伪的测量方法，无法区分真实耦合与设计伪影。

Method: 构建Contagion Tensor（三维张量：模态×智能体×时间步），导出CAF族指标（CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline]），采用bootstrap置信区间。设计2×2×2分块正交仿真，结合模态特异性消融实验，并在DeepSeek-Chat和GPT-4o-mini上开展真实API验证。

Result: 仿真中图像条件CAF=1.40（超线性），消融图像模块后降至0.87（亚线性），变化-0.53；文本条件无影响。真实API中，GPT-4o-mini视觉模态CAF=1.72（置信区间[1.700, 1.733]），文本模态CAF=1.02，验证了仿真的超线性预测。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1) 使输出分布耦合可定量证伪的测量工具；(2) 可迁移的消融协议，用于区分多智能体系统中的真实耦合与设计伪影。

Abstract: We introduce the Contagion Tensor, a measurement framework for quantifying how large language model (LLM) output distributions couple across modalities, agents, and time steps. From the tensor we derive the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF), a family of ratio-based metrics sharing the form CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline], providing unitless, baseline-referenced measurement with bootstrap confidence intervals. We instantiate CAF in four variants and evaluate the strongest in a complete 2x2x2 block-orthogonal simulation design with modality-specific ablation. The ablation reveals that an apparent image-condition super-linear effect (CAF = 1.40) collapses to sub-linear (CAF = 0.87) when the image perturbation module is disabled, a shift of -0.53 with zero effect on text conditions. We supplement with real-API experiments across two model families: DeepSeek-Chat (R=30) and GPT-4o-mini (R=15, real vision). Under uniform personas, text-only communication produces CAF approx 1.0 in both models. Diverse personas drive convergence (CAF = 0.88). A within-model comparison on GPT-4o-mini reveals: C3 (text) CAF = 1.02 vs. C5 (real vision, R=30) CAF = 1.72 [1.700, 1.733], delta = +0.70, validating the simulation's super-linear image-condition prediction. Of 11 conditions, 5 have been tested on real APIs and 6 remain unverified. Our contribution is two-layered: (1) a measurement instrument that makes output-distribution coupling quantitatively falsifiable; and (2) a transferable ablation protocol that any modular multi-agent simulator can adopt to distinguish genuine coupling from design artifacts.

</details>


### [171] [Analysis of Adam Algorithms for Stochastic Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28879)
*Xin Zheng,Yifei Jin,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Adam算法在时变非平稳随机系统中的通用理论，通过引入新的随机矩阵乘积分析技术和随机Lyapunov函数，推导了参数跟踪和输出预测误差边界，并提供了超参数选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Adam类算法的理论分析主要针对时不变模型参数且依赖i.i.d.数据假设，这严重违背了时变非平稳系统的实际特性，亟需突破传统理想化设定，建立适用于非平稳时变系统的理论框架。

Method: 提出了分析Adam耦合的一阶和二阶矩递归所导致的非平稳依赖随机矩阵乘积的新技术；构建了融合两种矩动力学的随机Lyapunov函数；在允许非平稳和依赖数据的随机激励条件下进行理论推导。

Result: 推导了参数跟踪误差和输出预测误差的显式界，定量刻画了步长、一阶/二阶动量参数、梯度噪声和参数漂移的影响；通过合成数据和真实数据实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅为Adam在非平稳系统中的应用提供了性能保证，还为超参数选择提供了指导原则，填补了Adam在时变非平稳设定下的理论空白。

Abstract: The adaptive moment estimation algorithm, known as Adam, is widely used in modern machine learning, owing to its low per-iteration complexity and strong empirical performance. Despite its prevalent use, the theoretical foundation of Adam remains largely unexplored for time-varying and nonstationary systems. In fact, the existing theoretical analyses of Adam-type algorithms are primarily concerned with time-invariant model parameters and explicitly or implicitly rely on independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data assumptions, under which the learning taskcan be formulated as minimizing a fixed expected objective with a static minimizer. However, such assumptions are often violated in time-varying and nonstationary systems, thereby calling for a theoretical investigation beyond the conventional yet idealized i.i.d. setting. The main objective of this paper is to solve this challenging problem by establishing a general theory of Adam for time-varying and nonstationary stochastic systems. We will introduce some new techniques for analyzing the products of nonstationary and dependent random matrices induced by Adam's coupled first- and second-moment recursions, and will construct a new stochastic Lyapunov function that blends these two moment dynamics. Under a stochastic excitation condition that allows nonstationary and dependent data, we will derive both parameter tracking and output prediction error bounds explicitly, quantifying the effects of stepsize, first- and second-momentum parameters, gradient noise and parameter drift. These bounds not only provide guarantees for Adam performance, but also provide guidelines for hyperparameter selection. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate our theory and design guidelines.

</details>


### [172] [An Integrated Machine Learning and Hierarchical Variance Decomposition Pipeline for Student Performance Prediction and Metacognitive Calibration on Multi-Signal Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28881)
*Gurdeep Singh Virdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了UBP-CAP统一框架，整合行为预测、校准分析与方差分解，并引入PEDI指标量化预测与解释的结构一致性。在1,195条记录上验证，Logistic回归预测最优(AUC=0.903)，学生存在系统性校准偏差(ECE=0.109)，校准更多是情境性而非特质性的(ICC=0.123)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将学生表现预测、校准误差计算和方差分解作为独立流程处理，缺乏统一解释框架，无法全面理解学生在智能辅导系统中的元认知校准行为。

Method: 提出UBP-CAP框架，包含三个模块：(1) LightGBM + SHAP用于二分类正确性预测；(2) ECE/MCE/Brier评分分解评估元认知校准；(3) 交叉广义线性混合效应模型(GLMM)分解校准偏差。同时引入PEDI量化预测与解释特征剖面间的结构差异。

Result: 在27名学生45个任务的1,195条交互记录上，Logistic回归AUC=0.903优于LightGBM(0.878)。学生朴素ECE(0.109)显著高于模型ECE(0.068)。交叉GLMM的ICCStudent=0.123，PEDIcos=0.081(p=0.327)表明预测与解释在共享行为特征上结构一致。

Conclusion: UBP-CAP实现了预测、校准与解释的统一分析，发现学生存在系统性过度自信偏差且校准行为更多依赖具体情境而非个人特质，预测模型与解释模型在行为特征层面具有结构一致性。

Abstract: Predicting student performance and characterizing metacognitive calibration are essential for personalization in intelligent tutoring systems. Prior research treats performance prediction, calibration error calculation, and variance decomposition as separate pipelines, preventing unified interpretation. I propose the Unified Behavioral Prediction and Calibration Analysis Pipeline (UBP-CAP), an integrated framework processing student pre-execution behavioral telemetry through three linked modules: (1) a LightGBM classifier with SHAP for binary correctness prediction, (2) formal calibration metrics (ECE, MCE, and Brier score decomposition) to evaluate metacognitive alignment, and (3) a crossed Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Model (GLMM) for decomposing calibration deviations. I introduce the Predictive-Explanatory Divergence Index (PEDI), which quantifies structural divergence between predictive and explanatory feature profiles. Evaluated on 1,195 interaction records (27 students, 45 tasks), Logistic Regression achieves AUC-ROC = 0.903, outperforming LightGBM (0.878). Student naive ECE (0.109) significantly exceeds model ECE (0.068), confirming systematic miscalibration. The crossed GLMM yields ICCStudent = 0.123, showing calibration is situational rather than dispositional. PEDIcos = 0.081 (p = 0.327) indicates structural alignment between prediction and explanation on shared behavioral features.

</details>


### [173] [ML-Powered LDAP Reconnaissance Detection using Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28917)
*Shaefer Drew,Edward Raff,Michael Brautbar,Yaron Zinar,Benjamin Malmberg,Dor Agron,Sagi Sheinfeld,Avraham Kama,Asaf Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对攻击者通过LDAP协议对Active Directory进行侦察的行为，提出了两种机器学习框架：一种基于弱监督的LDAP恶意查询分类器，另一种用于挖掘恶意查询签名的方法。分类器在保留集上达到65%的真阳性率，签名挖掘达到81.48%的字段精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDAP协议默认允许所有用户读取AD数据，攻击者在窃取身份后常利用LDAP进行初始侦察。为在侦察阶段早期捕获威胁行为者，需要自动化检测LDAP恶意查询的方法。

Method: 1) 通过关联LDAP查询与端点检测结果，使用弱监督方法标注大规模数据集，训练ML分类器区分恶意与良性LDAP查询；2) 在分类器基础上，利用严格的统计假设检验框架挖掘新颖的恶意LDAP签名。

Result: 分类器在保留集上达到65%的真阳性率（TPR），同时控制误报；挖掘的签名在CrowdStrike托管检测与响应团队评估中达到81.48%的字段精确率。

Conclusion: 弱监督方法虽在标注质量上不如人工标注，但能够利用大规模自动化语料构建降低成本和时间，在实际部署中更加实用，且两种方法均达到了可接受的性能基准。

Abstract: Lightweight Directory Access Protocol (LDAP) is a protocol that allows users to query and modify Active Directory (AD) data. By default, all users have read access to all AD data through LDAP, making it a common initial tool for reconnaissance when a threat actor first compromises an identity. To capture threat actors early in the reconnaissance phase, we developed two machine learning frameworks to detect LDAP reconnaissance: an ML classifier to predict malicious LDAP queries and an ML-based data-mining method to extract malicious query signatures. By correlating LDAP queries with endpoint detections, the first framework uses weak supervision to label a massive dataset and classify LDAP queries as malicious or benign. For immediate deployment, a second technique was developed on top of this approach to employ a rigorous statistical hypothesis-testing framework for mining novel, malicious LDAP signatures. While this weakly supervised approach is limited compared with manual human labeling, it is more practical for this use case because it leverages large-scale automated corpus construction, reducing costs and time. Ultimately, both the LDAP classifier and the ML-based LDAP signature mining method achieved performance benchmarks, with the classifier achieving up to a 65\% True Positive Rate (TPR) on the holdout set while limiting false positives, and mined signatures demonstrating 81.48\% field precision with CrowdStrike's Managed Detection and Response team.

</details>


### [174] [Towards Improved Anomaly Detection for Cloud Cybersecurity via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28923)
*Manu Nandan,TJ Jaymes,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的自监督学习方法，应用于AWS CloudTrail日志进行云安全威胁检测。该方法为每个事件生成异常评分，能动态适应组织变化而无需周期性重训练。在五个组织的实验中，该方法将告警量从传统方法的数千条/小时降至约1条/小时，但存在无法估计假阴性率的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云基础设施依赖增加，组织需要有效检测云环境中的安全威胁。传统基于启发式或异常检测的自动化威胁检测方法由于静态特性导致高误报率，需要更有效的方法来降低告警量并提高检测精度。

Method: 提出一种基于图神经网络的自监督学习方法，应用于AWS CloudTrail日志。模型为每个事件生成异常评分，能够动态适应组织变化而无需周期性重训练。在五个组织中进行实验评估，与基于领域专家规则的基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在五个组织的实验中，该方法在几乎所有情况下产生的告警量远少于基于领域专家规则的基线方法，将告警量从传统方法的数千条/小时降至约1条/小时。

Conclusion: 该方法能显著降低告警量，但评估仅涵盖已标记事件，无法从当前数据估计假阴性率，因此结果应被视为实际部署研究而非完全验证的检测系统。未来工作需扩展至其他云环境并解决当前局限性。

Abstract: Detecting security threats in an organization's cloud computing environment has become necessary due to the increased reliance on cloud infrastructure. Logging of all cloud computing events enables investigation into any incidents after they are detected. Automated detection of threats using the logs based on heuristics or anomaly detection could result in a high false positive rate due to its relatively static nature. In this article, we present an industrial case study of a self-supervised learning method using graph neural networks applied to AWS CloudTrail logs to surface suspicious events for analyst review. The model produces an anomaly score for each event and dynamically adapts to changes in the organization without requiring periodic retraining. Based on our experiments across five organizations, the proposed model produced substantially fewer alerts than a domain expert rule-based baseline in almost all cases, reducing alert volumes to approximately 1 per hour from thousands generated by traditional methods. We note that this evaluation covers only flagged events, and false negatives cannot be estimated from the current data; findings should therefore be interpreted as a practical deployment study offering insights into real-world constraints rather than a fully validated detection system. We discuss these limitations and the requirements for extending the approach to other cloud environments as future work.

</details>


### [175] [Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925)
*Ananto Nayan Bala,Faisal Muhammad Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个固定目录多智能体路由基准（含3,000条提示、12个智能体），系统比较多种路由方法，发现监督式方法显著优于零样本方法，并引入加权路由层以优化准确率与成本的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示下的工具和智能体路由本质上是一个集合预测问题：单个查询可能需要多个智能体，而过量选择会增加执行成本，因此需要系统性的基准测试来评估准确率与成本之间的权衡。

Method: 从WildChat衍生出3,000条提示，覆盖12个智能体的固定目录，采用AI辅助启发式标注和受控重平衡以支持多标签评估。评估协议包括集合级指标（精确率、召回率、F1、Jaccard、精确匹配）、延迟、执行导向的能力覆盖模拟，以及基于序数智能体成本层的约束加权路由设置。比较方法包括最近邻匹配、线性多标签分类、依赖感知基线、微调编码器、确定性加权后评分（WAR）以及零样本LLM基线。

Result: 监督式路由器大幅优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由；微调编码器在无约束集合准确率上最强；线性多标签模型是最强实用基线；在约束设置下，加权路由层在强监督评分器之上能提升效用，Encoder+WAR增益最大。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个基于自然语言提示的固定目录多智能体路由基准测试，实验表明监督式路由方法显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由，其中微调编码器在无约束设置下表现最佳，线性多标签模型则提供了最强实用基线，而加权路由层在约束设置下能有效提升效用。

Abstract: Tool and agent routing from natural-language prompts is naturally a set-valued prediction problem: a single query may require multiple agents, while over-selection increases execution cost. The benchmark introduced here is derived from WildChat and contains 3,000 prompts over a fixed 12-agent catalog, with AI-assisted heuristic labels under a fixed schema and controlled rebalancing for multi-label evaluation. The evaluation protocol combines set-level metrics (Precision, Recall, F1, Jaccard, and Exact Match), latency, an execution-oriented capability-coverage simulation, and a constrained weighted-routing setting based on ordinal agent-cost tiers. Compared methods include nearest-neighbor matching, linear multilabel classification, dependency-aware baselines, a fine-tuned encoder, deterministic weighted post-scoring via Weighted Agent Routing (WAR), and a zero-shot LLM baseline. Results show that supervised routers substantially outperform nearest-neighbor and zero-shot LLM routing. The fine-tuned encoder achieves the strongest unconstrained set accuracy, while the linear multilabel model provides the strongest practical baseline. In the constrained setting, the weighted routing layer improves utility when applied on top of strong supervised scorers, with the largest gain observed for Encoder+WAR. Overall, the benchmark and evaluation protocol support reproducible study of accuracy-cost trade-offs in fixed-catalog multi-agent routing.

</details>


### [176] [DLR: Zero-Inference-Cost Latent Residuals for Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28932)
*Dong Wang,Wenwu Tang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR)方法，通过无参数的可折叠插件增强低秩预训练，在不增加部署成本的情况下提升模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型预训练成本过高，低秩预训练虽能降低参数和FLOPs但质量通常不及全秩训练，需要一种方法在保持低秩训练效率的同时提升性能。

Method: DLR在标准低秩输出Bz上添加固定的结构化残差alpha/sqrt(K) * Expand_K(z)，将每个潜在坐标复制K=ceil(d_out/r)次；训练后通过闭式吸收到上投影矩阵B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T中，不增加部署参数。

Result: 在60M到7B参数的LLaMA模型上，DLR在多数设置下提升了C4验证集困惑度，130M以上参数增益最明显；折叠后的检查点可无缝迁移到标准监督微调基准。

Conclusion: DLR是一种训练时有效、部署时零开销的低秩预训练增强方法，能显著缩小低秩与全秩训练之间的质量差距。

Abstract: Large language models have driven recent progress in language and multimodal AI, yet pre-training them at scale is prohibitively expensive. Low-rank pre-training, which factorizes each weight matrix into a rank-r product to reduce both parameters and FLOPs, is a promising response but typically lags full-rank training in quality. We propose Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR), a training-only, parameter-free, foldable plug-in for low-rank pre-training. DLR augments the standard low-rank output Bz with a fixed structured residual alpha/sqrt(K) * Expand_K(z) that replicates each latent coordinate K = ceil(d_out/r) times across the output. With alpha fixed, DLR adds zero learnable parameters per layer; after training, it is absorbed into the up-projection in closed form, B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T, so deployment parameter count, FLOPs and memory match the underlying low-rank backbone exactly. Across LLaMA models from 60M to 7B parameters, DLR strengthens low-rank pre-training on C4 validation perplexity in most settings, with the clearest gains at 130M and above; folded checkpoints transfer cleanly to supervised fine-tuning on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [177] [ReGuide: From Test-Time Guidance to Self-Improving Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28939)
*Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGuide是一个自改进框架，通过相位条件引导（PCG）生成可纠正的轨迹回滚，并将其作为可重用的在线恢复数据来微调或重训练行为克隆扩散策略，从而有效应对协变量偏移问题，在多个机器人操作任务上提升成功率1.3-7.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆扩散策略虽然表达能力强，但易受协变量偏移影响——与示范状态的微小偏差会累积导致任务失败。现有方法要么通过专家修正或数据增强扩展训练分布（成本高或依赖假设），要么在测试时用学习模型引导冻结策略（丢弃纠正轨迹），均存在局限。

Method: 提出ReGuide框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG)：构建相位特定的潜在目标，仅在漂移但可恢复区域施加引导，通过估计清洁动作匹配动力学模型的训练分布来生成纠正性回滚；2) ReGuide-FT（微调当前检查点）和ReGuide-FS（在增强数据集上从头训练）两种吸收纠正轨迹的方式，可组合和迭代使用。

Result: 在Robomimic的Can、Square、Transport和Tool Hang四个任务上，ReGuide将基础策略成功率提升1.3-7.7倍，在仅测试时引导的设置中优于LPB，消融实验表明性能提升来自引导恢复数据而非额外回滚本身。

Conclusion: ReGuide通过将引导回滚作为可重用的在线恢复数据，有效解决了行为克隆策略的协变量偏移问题，提供了一种无需额外专家干预或复杂数据增强的自改进方案。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned diffusion policies are expressive but remain vulnerable to covariate shift: small deviations from demonstrated states can compound into task failure. Existing methods address this either by expanding the training distribution through expert corrections or synthetic augmentation, or by steering a frozen policy at test time with guidance from a learned model. The former can be expensive or assumption-dependent, while the latter discards the corrected trajectories after execution. We introduce ReGuide, a self-improving framework that treats guided rollouts as reusable on-policy recovery data. ReGuide first uses Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG) to generate corrective rollouts: it constructs phase-specific latent targets, applies guidance only in the drifted-but-recoverable regime, and guides through the estimated clean action to match the dynamics model's training distribution. Successful guided rollouts are then absorbed back into the policy through ReGuide-FT, which fine-tunes the current checkpoint, or ReGuide-FS, which retrains from scratch on the augmented dataset; the two can also be composed and iterated. On Robomimic Can, Square, Transport, and Tool Hang, ReGuide improves base-policy success by $1.3$--$7.7\times$, outperforms LPB in the test-time-only setting, and matched-data ablations show that the gains come from guided recovery data rather than additional rollouts alone.

</details>


### [178] [Machine-learnable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28947)
*Veit Elser,Manish Krishan Lal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化定义，描述具有易识别、易生成且易从样例学习三大特性的大规模离散集合，并通过布尔自编码器实现机器可学习性，实验展示了Rorschach模式及可迭代演化的"野生"集合的可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在形式化定义一类具有易识别、易生成且易从样例学习特性的离散集合，并探索其机器可学习性的理论基础与实现方法。

Method: 将问题特化到二进制字符串集合，基于存在有界复杂度的布尔自编码器（固定集合元素）来定义"机器可学习性"，并通过布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器进行实验验证。

Result: Rorschach模式（镜像半区可反转对比度）展示了机器可学习性；更"野生"的集合仅能被自编码器近似固定，但通过简单迭代可使这些集合演化变得可学习。

Conclusion: 证明了布尔自编码器框架下某些离散集合的可学习性，并展示了野生集合可通过迭代演化为可学习集合。

Abstract: In this study we present a formal definition of large discrete sets having, informally, three properties: their elements are easily recognized, easily generated, and the latter tasks are easily learned from examples. The formalism is specialized to sets of binary strings and a definition of "machine-learnability" based on the existence of a bounded-complexity Boolean autoencoder that fixes the elements of the set. We present experiments where the autoencoders are implemented by nets of Boolean threshold functions. Machine-learnability is demonstrated for Rorschach patterns (that may have reversed contrast in the mirrored half), and considerably "wilder" sets whose elements are only approximately fixed by admissible autoencoders. In the second case we demonstrate a simple iteration that evolves wild sets to make them properly machine-learnable.

</details>


### [179] [Modification-Considering Value Learning for Reward Hacking Mitigation in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28955)
*Evgenii Opryshko,Umangi Jain,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCVL方法，通过过滤可能被利用的奖励信号来防御奖励黑客攻击，在防止奖励欺骗的同时允许策略持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体会利用错误设定的奖励信号获得高表观回报但实际失败，现有防御方法在抑制奖励黑客与允许合法改进之间存在矛盾。

Method: MCVL包装离策略学习器，将每个传入的转移视为候选修改，分别预测包含和不包含该转移的两条训练路径，并用冻结的自举回报估计器评分，仅当不降低分数时接收该转移。

Result: 在四个安全相关网格世界和三个修改过的MuJoCo连续控制任务中，MCVL在缓解奖励黑客的同时持续改进预期目标。

Conclusion: MCVL通过形式化过滤条件实现了既安全又宽松的防御，在标准值型RL中有效解决了奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents can exploit misspecified reward signals to achieve high apparent returns while failing on the intended objective, a failure mode known as reward hacking. Existing practical defenses typically constrain policy updates to stay near a known safe reference, creating a tension between suppressing hacking and permitting legitimate improvement. We propose Modification-Considering Value Learning (MCVL), which operationalizes the theoretical idea of current utility optimization for standard value-based RL. MCVL wraps an off-policy learner and treats each incoming transition as a candidate modification: it forecasts two training paths, one that includes the transition and one that does not, and scores both with a frozen bootstrapped-return estimator derived from a learned reward model and value function. The transition is admitted only if inclusion does not decrease the score. We formalize conditions under which this filtering is both safe and permissive, and instantiate MCVL with DDQN and TD3. Across four safety-relevant gridworlds and three modified MuJoCo continuous-control tasks with diverse hacking mechanisms, MCVL mitigates reward hacking while continuing to improve the intended objective. Project website: ktolnos.github.io/mcvl/.

</details>


### [180] [RGLD: Randomized Global-Local Density Estimation for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28970)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Ye Tian,Josh Victoria,Zhijun Wang,Pietro Mercati,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGLD，一种结合全局随机特征密度分支和局部邻域分支的无监督表格异常检测方法，在47个数据集上超越了23种基线方法，AUROC排名第一，AUPRC排名第二，速度比深度检测器快50-580倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督表格异常检测方法存在两难：经典统计方法高效但依赖固定数据视图和单一异常概念；深度方法可学习更灵活的打分函数但速度慢、无监督下难以调参。需要一种兼具准确率、鲁棒性和计算效率的方法。

Method: RGLD包含两个分支：(1) 全局随机特征密度分支——识别位于全局低密度区域的样本；(2) 局部邻域分支——检测缺乏邻近观测支持的样本。两个分支均在特征装袋的随机视图上运行，以暴露单一表示中可能隐藏的异常证据。

Result: 在47个表格数据集上对比23种统计和深度基线，RGLD的AUROC数据集级性能排名第一，AUPRC胜场排名第二；速度比所有深度检测器快50-580倍，与统计方法运行时间相当。

Conclusion: RGLD在准确率和效率之间取得了优越的权衡，为无监督表格异常检测提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised tabular anomaly detection requires methods that are accurate, robust across heterogeneous datasets, and computationally efficient. Classical statistical detectors are often efficient, but they usually rely on a fixed data view and a single notion of abnormality. Deep anomaly detectors can learn more flexible scoring functions, but they are substantially slower and difficult to tune in unsupervised settings due to the lack of a reliable supervisory signal. We propose RGLD, a randomized global-local density estimator for efficient unsupervised tabular anomaly detection. RGLD combines a global random-feature density branch, which identifies samples in broadly low-density regions, with a local neighbor branch, which detects samples that are weakly supported by nearby observations. Both branches operate over feature-bagged randomized views, allowing RGLD to expose anomaly evidence that may be hidden in any single representation. We conduct experiments on 47 tabular datasets against 23 statistical and deep anomaly detection baselines under fully unsupervised setting. RGLD achieves the strongest dataset-level AUROC performance, ranking 1st in dataset wins, and ranks 2nd in AUPRC wins. RGLD is also faster than all evaluated deep detectors, achieving 50x-580x speedups, and remains competitive with statistical methods in runtime, yielding a favorable accuracy-efficiency tradeoff.

</details>


### [181] [On Surrogate Modeling of Static Response of AM Short-Fiber Thermoplastics Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28996)
*Pharindra Pathak,Vipin Kumar,Trenton M. Ricks,Suhasini Gururaja,Siddhartha Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合图神经网络与长短期记忆网络的混合代理模型，用于预测短纤维热塑性复合材料的力学行为，计算成本降低两个数量级以上，预测精度R²≈0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短纤维热塑性复合材料因其轻质、高强度和可回收性在航空航天和汽车领域应用广泛，但其力学行为受纤维取向、空间聚集和制造孔隙率等介观尺度特征显著影响，且存在空间变异性。虽然介观有限元模型能解析这种异质性，但对真实三维微结构的计算成本过高，亟需一种高效且准确的替代方法。

Method: 基于微CT数据重建微结构，离散为Voronoi单元以表征纤维相互作用邻域；通过非线性有限元模拟（含基体损伤）对各单元进行均质化，生成应力-应变数据；构建混合图神经网络-长短期记忆网络架构，编码微结构拓扑与历史相关的力学演化。

Result: 代理模型对未见微结构的刚度和应力-应变行为预测精度R²≈0.98，计算成本相比高保真有限元模拟降低两个数量级以上；耦合实验标定的损伤定律后发现，纤维取向、聚集和孔隙率共同主导局部有效刚度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条物理信息驱动、数据高效的路径，用于识别力学薄弱微结构单元，加速短纤维热塑性复合材料组件的数字孪生开发。

Abstract: Short-fiber thermoplastic (SFT) composites are increasingly employed in lightweight aerospace and automotive structures owing to their favorable strength-to-weight ratio, high production rates, and recyclability. Unlike continuous-fiber systems, the mechanical response of SFTs is governed by mesoscale interactions among fiber orientation, spatial clustering, and manufacturing-induced porosity. These features exhibit significant spatial variability in manufactured components and influence stiffness, damage initiation, and nonlinear deformation. Although mesoscale finite element (FE) models can resolve such heterogeneity, their application to realistic three-dimensional microstructures remains computationally intractable.
  A data-driven surrogate framework is proposed to predict the mechanical behavior of additively manufactured, compression-molded (AM-CM) SFTs. Microstructures reconstructed from micro-computed tomography data were discretized into Voronoi-based cells representing distinct fiber-interaction neighborhoods. Each cell was homogenized via nonlinear FE simulations incorporating matrix damage, and the resulting stress-strain responses trained a hybrid Graph Neural Network-Long Short-Term Memory (GNN-LSTM) architecture encoding microstructural topology and history-dependent mechanical evolution.
