### [326] [SWE-INTERACT: Reimagining SWE Benchmarks as User-Driven Long-Horizon Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30573)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Anisha Gunjal,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Interact 是一个用于评估编码智能体在多轮、交互式、用户驱动的软件工程任务中的新测试基准。与现有单轮 SWE 基准不同，它通过用户模拟器逐步揭示需求、提供反馈和修订，测试智能体发现用户意图、适应变化需求的能力。实验发现，强模型在单轮任务中表现优秀但多轮任务大幅下降（从50%降至25%），最强模型（如Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5）在模糊指令下仍能坚持并整合需求，但存在过度自主编码、遗忘需求等技术缺陷；弱模型则在模糊性下表现更差、更早放弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿 SWE 基准（如 SWE-bench）通常在任务开始时提供完整需求，评估智能体的自主实现能力。然而，现实中的软件开发流程往往是用户逐步提出需求、提供反馈和修订的迭代过程。作者希望构建一个更贴近真实开发工作流的评估测试床，衡量智能体在交互式、多轮、用户驱动的场景下的能力，填补现有基准的空白。

Method: 设计了 SWE-Interact 测试床，包含一个精心设计的用户模拟器。该模拟器从模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查智能体的工作空间，并提供有针对性的反馈、修订和新约束，直到完整任务目标被传达。测试床基于大规模真实编码智能体交互研究构建，评估了多种前沿和开源模型在多轮交互任务中的表现。

Result: 强模型在单轮 SWE 任务上可解决约50%的任务，但在对应的 SWE-Interact 多轮交互任务中仅解决约25%。最强模型（Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5）在模糊初始指令下仍能坚持，整合需求并编写干净代码，但存在过度自主编码、遗忘需求和技术错误等问题。弱模型在模糊性下表现更差，更早放弃，容易忽略指令并频繁重写代码。

Conclusion: SWE-Interact 衡量了一个正交的、面向现实世界能力的维度：交互式目标发现和与用户迭代精化的能力。单轮 SWE 任务上的强性能并不能可靠地迁移到多轮、用户驱动的工作流中，这为前沿模型开发提供了一个重要的评估方向。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Interact, a new testbed for evaluating coding agents on multi-turn, interactive, user-driven software engineering tasks. Existing frontier SWE benchmarks typically provide complete requirements upfront and evaluate agents on autonomous implementation. In contrast, SWE-Interact places agents in a realistic developer workflow: a carefully designed user simulator starts with vague or incomplete instructions, progressively reveals requirements, inspects the agent's workspace, and provides targeted feedback, revisions, and new constraints until the full task goal has been handed off. Grounded in large-scale studies of real coding-agent interactions, this setup tests whether agents can discover user intent, adapt to evolving requirements, and build on their own prior work. Across a suite of frontier and open-weight models, we find that strong performance on single-turn SWE tasks does not reliably transfer to multi-turn, user-driven workflows: the best-performing models solve roughly 50% of single-turn baseline tasks but only 25% of the corresponding SWE-Interact tasks. The strongest models in our evaluation, including Opus 4.8 and GPT 5.5, start strong even in the face of vague initial instructions, persevere until all the requirements are surfaced by the user, integrate them better and write clean code. However, they still suffer from over-agentic coding, forgetting requirements and technical mistakes. Weaker models start poorly under ambiguity, give up early, forget or ignore instructions and rework their code more. Overall, SWE-Interact measures an orthogonal, real-world capability axis for frontier model development: interactive goal discovery and iterative refinement with a user in the loop.

</details>


### [327] [The Fundamental Limits of Valid Transport Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30574)
*Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在极小极大框架下正式定义了任意有效传输映射的估计问题，证明在标准稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射与估计最优传输映射在统计上同样困难；但当稳定性假设不成立时，非最优映射可被更准确地学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现代生成模型（如扩散模型、归一化流、流匹配）估计的是传输映射，但并未明确针对最优传输（OT）映射。由于生成建模中传输成本本身无关紧要，自然希望关注更易处理的映射。然而，这些方法在统计上的样本复杂度缺乏严格的理论分析，作者希望通过极小极大框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用极小极大统计决策理论框架，将传输映射估计问题形式化为一个统计推断问题；结合OT文献中的标准稳定性假设（如Lipschitz连续性等），推导样本复杂度下界；并通过反例说明当稳定性条件不满足时，非最优映射可达到更低的估计误差。

Result: 1) 在标准稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射的样本复杂度下界与估计OT映射的样本复杂度下界相同，即两者统计上一样困难；2) 当稳定性假设不成立时，存在实例表明非最优传输映射可以被显著更准确地学习。

Conclusion: 极小极大框架为理解基于传输的现代生成方法的统计极限提供了严格基础，阐明了在何种条件下追求次优映射能带来真实的统计优势，也为扩散模型、流匹配等方法的样本复杂度下界提供了统一的理论视角。

Abstract: Many modern generative modeling methods, including diffusion models, normalizing flows, and flow matching, estimate transport maps or plans between distributions without explicitly targeting an optimal transport (OT) map. In applications like generative modeling, the transport cost itself is irrelevant, and this makes it natural to target maps which are more tractable from either a statistical or computational standpoint. In this short note, we formalize the task of estimating any valid transport map in a rigorous minimax framework. One consequence of this framing is that it yields sample complexity lower bounds for any method whose learned object is evaluated as a transport map or plan, including flow matching and diffusion-based generative models, in settings where direct analysis would be challenging due to the analytic complexity of the methods and their target maps. We observe that, under standard, though strong, stability assumptions from the OT literature, estimating any valid transport map is statistically as hard as estimating the OT map. We complement these results with some examples showing that when these stability assumptions fail, alternative transport maps can be learned substantially more accurately than the OT map. Our minimax framing provides a rigorous foundation for understanding the statistical limits of modern transport-based generative methods and clarifies when targeting sub-optimal maps can provide real statistical advantages.

</details>


### [328] [C$^{2}$R: Cross-sample Consistency Regularization Mitigates Feature Splitting and Absorption in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30609)
*Haoran Jin,Xiting Wang,Shijie Ren,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R）方法，通过惩罚批次中方向相似潜在变量的共激活，有效缓解稀疏自编码器中的特征分裂和特征吸收问题，提升潜在变量可解释性而不牺牲重构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）在扩展字典规模时面临特征分裂（连贯概念被碎片化为非原子潜在变量）和特征吸收（通用特征中产生任意例外）两大根本性挑战，严重损害了潜在变量的可靠性，亟需有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R），核心思想是显式鼓励每个语义特征在批次内由统一的潜在变量一致表示，具体通过惩罚方向相似的潜在变量在同一批次内共激活来实现，从而引入跨样本约束以解决传统SAE中缺乏此类约束导致的潜在分配不一致问题。

Result: C²R有效缓解了特征分裂和特征吸收问题，同时保持了重构保真度，验证了该方法在不降低模型性能的前提下提升潜在变量可解释性的有效性。

Conclusion: C²R为SAE的可解释性问题提供了一种原则性解决方案，通过引入跨样本一致性约束从根本上解决了潜在分配不一致的缺陷，为大规模语言模型的可解释性研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret large language models by decomposing activations into sparse, human-understandable features, but scaling to large dictionaries exposes fundamental challenges. Systematic studies reveal pervasive feature splitting that fragments coherent concepts into non-atomic latents and widespread feature absorption that creates arbitrary exceptions in general features, severely compromising latent reliability. These issues stem from inconsistent latent assignment across samples: without cross-sample constraints, per-sample optimization often allows a single underlying concept to be inconsistently distributed across multiple redundant or interfering latents. To address this, we introduce C$^2$R (\underline{\textbf{C}}ross-sample \underline{\textbf{C}}onsistency \underline{\textbf{R}}egularization). C$^2$R explicitly encourages that each semantic feature is consistently represented by a unified latent across the batch by penalizing the co-activation of directionally similar latents. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that C$^2$R effectively mitigates both splitting and absorption while, crucially, preserving reconstruction fidelity, providing a principled solution that enhances latent interpretability without degrading model performance. Source code is available at https://github.com/hr-jin/Cross-sample-Consistency-Regularization.

</details>


### [329] [Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现离线保守训练（高β DPO）反而会加剧在线适配时的奖励黑客行为，并提出需采用**校准的**而非**最大化的**保守主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"离线保守训练能提供安全基础、减少奖励模型利用"这一广泛直觉，实证检验保守主义与奖励黑客之间的关系。

Method: 使用Qwen3-14B策略在三档保守度（β_lo, β_mid, β_hi）下进行DPO训练，随后对各检查点在线适配奖励集成（3×Qwen3-1.7B），基于GSM8K准确率测量真实性能。

Result: 高保守度单调增加奖励黑客损害（Goodhart差距、AUGC），Spearman ρ=1.0。机制链：高β压缩策略熵→输出多样性降低→奖励模型训练分布集中→但集合分歧（认知不确定性）增大且被更快利用。

Conclusion: 需采用**校准的**而非**最大化的**保守主义，存在最优保守度β*平衡对齐保真度与黑客脆弱性。

Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($β\in \{β_{\mathrm{lo}}, β_{\mathrm{mid}}, β_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$β$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $β$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(β, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $β^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


### [330] [One-Step Gradient Delay is Not a Barrier for Large-Scale Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30634)
*Philip Zmushko,Egor Petrov,Nursultan Abdullaev,Mikhail Khrushchev,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了异步流水线并行中一步梯度延迟必然导致训练不稳定的普遍认知，证明延迟影响高度依赖优化器选择（AdamW退化严重而Muon鲁棒性强），并提出基于错误反馈的修正方法，在10B参数模型上实现了与同步训练相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步流水线并行（如PipeDream-2BW）能消除同步流水线中的气泡浪费，但因其引入梯度陈旧性问题被认为本质上不稳定而未被广泛采用。本文旨在挑战这一假设，证明一步延迟下的性能退化并非固有缺陷，而是取决于优化器选择。

Method: 1) 对多种优化器（AdamW、Muon等）在一步梯度延迟下的鲁棒性进行全面的实证对比分析；2) 提出一种与优化器无关的基于错误反馈（Error Feedback）的修正方法以进一步缓解延迟效应；3) 提供Muon在有/无该修正下的收敛性理论分析。

Result: AdamW在一步延迟下性能严重退化，而Muon表现出强鲁棒性；所提出的错误反馈修正方法能进一步弥合与同步训练的性能差距；在10B参数模型上的实验验证了策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 异步流水线并行在大规模LLM训练中具有实际应用潜力，优化器选择是决定其对梯度陈旧性敏感度的关键因素，结合合适的优化器与误差反馈修正可消除与同步训练的性能差距。

Abstract: Modern large-scale LLM pretraining benefits from utilizing Pipeline Parallelism; however, synchronous implementations leave GPUs idle during pipeline bubbles, wasting computational resources. Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism eliminates these bubbles, maximizing throughput at the cost of gradient staleness. Among asynchronous schedules, PipeDream-2BW is particularly appealing: unlike the original PipeDream schedule, it ensures a constant one-step gradient delay regardless of pipeline depth. However, its adoption remains limited due to the common belief that optimizing under staleness is fundamentally unstable. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that degradation under one-step delay depends strongly on optimizer choice rather than being an intrinsic limitation. We provide the first comprehensive empirical analysis showing that while AdamW, the predominant optimizer at the time when PipeDream-2BW was introduced, indeed suffers from severe degradation, recent methods like Muon exhibit strong robustness under a one-step delay. We introduce an optimizer-agnostic Error Feedback-inspired correction to further mitigate delay effects. We provide supporting theoretical analysis demonstrating convergence for Muon with and without this correction. Extensive evaluation on models up to 10B parameters confirms that our strategies bridge the performance gap with synchronous training, highlighting the practical potential of asynchronous pipeline parallelism at scale.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [Unit-Circle Moment Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28894)
*Yu Su,Yao Wang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解析延拓的单位圆矩闭合方法，通过Takagi-Prony过程从映射生成函数的有效极点结构重建未解析的高阶矩，无需预设分布形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩闭合方法通常预设分布形式或直接截断矩层级，这在强非高斯状态下会导致不准确或不稳定，亟需一种无需固定分布假设的通用闭合方案。

Method: 将原始矩映射到有界的单位圆矩，利用映射生成函数的极点结构，通过Takagi-Prony过程重建未解析的高阶矩，实现解析延拓形式的矩闭合。

Result: 静、动态算例均表明该方法能准确重建非高斯分布，并实现矩层级的稳定演化。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种基于解析结构而非直接截断的矩闭合通用视角，为随机、动力学和量子系统的降阶描述开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Moment closure is a central problem in reduced descriptions of stochastic, kinetic, and quantum dynamics, where equations for low-order observables are coupled to an unresolved hierarchy of higher-order moments. Existing closures usually impose a prescribed form on the distribution or directly truncate the hierarchy, which can become inaccurate or unstable for strongly non-Gaussian states. Here we introduce unit-circle moment closure, which recasts the problem as analytic continuation. Raw moments are mapped to bounded unit-circle moments, whose unresolved tail is reconstructed by a Takagi-Prony procedure from the effective pole structure of a mapped generating function. The resulting continuation yields stable higher-order moments without assuming a fixed distributional ansatz. Illustrative static and dynamical examples demonstrate accurate reconstruction of non-Gaussian distributions and stable evolution of moment hierarchies. Our approach provides a general perspective for moment closure based on analytic structure rather than direct truncation.

</details>


### [332] [Latent Genetic Algorithm for Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29220)
*Kaixin Zheng,Wanjian Yin,Hongyu Yu,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种潜变量遗传算法（LGA），利用预训练通用原子间势的潜在表示作为连续进化坐标进行晶体结构预测。LGA通过逆优化匹配父代潜在向量的插值目标，显著提高了HfO₂基态恢复率（从20-35%提升至60-95%），降低了搜索成本近十倍，并在(PbTiO₃)ₙ/(PbZrO₃)ₙ超晶格中发现了一种未报道过的√2×3√2×1长周期基态结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体结构预测面临能量景观崎岖不平的挑战，实空间交叉操作往往会破坏父代结构中的有利局部构型，导致搜索效率低下。现有方法在处理不同晶胞、密度和对称性的候选结构时难以有效继承有益结构基元。

Method: LGA利用预训练通用原子间势学习到的潜在表示作为连续进化坐标，通过逆优化原子位置和晶格矢量来匹配父代潜在向量插值得到的目标潜在表示，从而生成子代结构。该方法抑制了高能量和短接触子代结构，并支持统一的变超胞搜索。

Result: LGA将HfO₂的基态恢复率从20-35%提升至60-95%，对16种钙钛矿的搜索成本降低了近十倍。在(PbTiO₃)ₙ/(PbZrO₃)ₙ超晶格中发现了√2×3√2×1长周期基态结构，其特征为共有的面内有限q调制q∥=(1/6,1/6)及层耦合边带，该面内周期为此前未见报道。

Conclusion: LGA提供了一种表示引导的基态结构预测新范式，为材料逆向设计提供了一条实用且无需解码器的有效途径。

Abstract: Predicting crystal structures requires navigating rugged energy landscapes in which favorable local motifs must be inherited across candidates with incompatible cells, densities, and symmetries. Conventional real-space crossover often destroys these motifs when parent structures are geometrically mismatched. Here we show that latent representations learned by pretrained universal interatomic potentials can serve as continuous evolutionary coordinates for crystal structure prediction. In the Latent Genetic Algorithm (LGA), offspring are generated by inverse optimization of atomic positions and lattice vectors to match a target latent representation, which is constructed via interpolation of the parent latent vectors. LGA suppresses high-energy and short-contact offspring, increases the HfO$_2$ ground-state recovery rate from 20-35% to 60-95%, and enables a unified variable-supercell search over 16 perovskites with a nearly tenfold reduction in search cost. Applied to (PbTiO$_3$)$_n$/(PbZrO$_3$)$_n$ superlattices, LGA reveals $\sqrt{2} \times 3\sqrt{2} \times 1$ long-period ground-state structures characterized by a common in-plane finite-$q$ modulation $q{_\parallel} = (1/6,1/6)$ and layer-coupled sidebands. To our knowledge, this in-plane periodicity has not been reported in any related oxide perovskite superlattice studies. Altogether, LGA offers a powerful representation-guided paradigm for ground-state structure prediction and provides a practical, decoder-free route toward materials inverse design.

</details>


### [333] [Verified residual-specific explicit derivative kernels for physics-informed learning and discretized PDE adjoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29702)
*Wenbo Cao,Zhe Lu,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体辅助实现和严格数值验证的显式微分（ED）方法，作为科学计算中通用自动微分（AD）的结构感知替代方案。对于PINNs，残差专用偏导喷射传播实现了2-4倍训练加速并降低GPU内存；对于CFD离散伴随，生成的正切和转置核通过一致性检验，成功用于马赫数和攻角反演。验证后的显式微分核可作为导数密集型科学计算中实用、结构感知的AD补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用自动微分（AD）在高阶残差和复杂离散算子中会产生显著的运行时和内存开销，而显式导数内核虽能利用问题特定结构实现高效、可控求导，但其推导和实现成本长期限制了应用。

Method: 提出残差专用的偏导喷射传播方法，实现目标PDE残差的导数状态显式闭合，通过专用逐层内核替代嵌套AD或通用泰勒模式变换；对CFD有限体积残差，采用相同验证驱动策略生成正切作用和转置作用核，并通过泰勒余数、内积和简化梯度一致性检验。

Result: ED核与嵌套AD达到浮点精度级别的一致性；完整PINN训练实现2-4倍加速并降低峰值GPU内存；CFD离散伴随流程成功实现自由流马赫数和攻角反演。

Conclusion: 验证后的显式导数内核在智能体辅助实现支持下，可作为通用AD在导数密集型科学计算中的实用、结构感知补充方案。

Abstract: Derivative computation is central to scientific computing, from space-time derivatives in physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) to residual Jacobian actions and discrete-adjoint operators in computational fluid dynamics (CFD). General-purpose automatic differentiation (AD) reduces implementation effort, but can incur substantial runtime and memory overhead for high-order residuals and complex discretized operators. Explicit derivative kernels can exploit problem-specific structure and provide efficient, controllable evaluations, but their use has been limited by derivation and implementation costs. This work revisits explicit differentiation (ED) as a residual-specific and verifiable route enabled by agent-assisted implementation and stringent numerical verification. For PINNs, we propose residual-specific partial-jet propagation, which makes the derivative-state closure of the target PDE residual explicit and realizes it through specialized layerwise kernels, rather than relying only on nested AD or a generic Taylor-mode transform. Relative to nested AD, the resulting ED kernels achieve floating-point-level agreement in residual and parameter-gradient evaluations and accelerate complete PINN training, often reaching 2-4x speedups while reducing peak GPU memory in most cases. For discretized PDE adjoints, we apply the same verification-driven strategy to a finite-volume CFD residual. The generated tangent-action and transpose-action kernels pass Taylor-remainder, inner-product, and reduced-gradient consistency checks, and are embedded into a GPU-resident discrete-adjoint workflow for freestream Mach-number and angle-of-attack inversion. These results suggest that verified explicit derivative kernels, supported by agent-assisted implementation, can serve as a practical, structure-aware complement to general-purpose AD for derivative-intensive scientific computing.

</details>


### [334] [High-order tensor neural network for iteration-free structure relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29919)
*Shaobo Yu,Haoting Zhang,Yu Han,Zhennan Zhang,Zhiyue Guo,Junjie Wang,Hao Gao,Jian Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: HotRelax是一种基于高阶张量消息传递神经网络的方法，能够仅通过一次前向传播从非弛豫结构直接预测弛豫结构，无需DFT力标签、迭代优化或后处理，在多种材料体系上表现出色，可有效加速高通量材料发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从头算结构弛豫是高通量材料筛选中的主要瓶颈，而现有机器学习势虽能加速但仍需迭代优化和高质量DFT力标签，效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出HotRelax，采用高阶张量消息传递神经网络，直接在成对的非弛豫和弛豫结构上训练，实现端到端的单次前向传播预测弛豫结构，无需DFT力标签、迭代推理或后处理。

Result: 在3D体相晶体、2D层状材料和催化剂等五个数据集上，HotRelax在多项基准上取得了更低的预测误差，模型体积紧凑且推理高效。DFT验证表明预测结构与DFT弛豫结构能量接近。在催化流程中，HotRelax还提升了弛豫态能量预测模型的准确性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HotRelax是一种高效且广泛适用的端到端结构弛豫框架，有潜力显著加速高通量材料发现。

Abstract: Structure relaxation is important for the discovery of new materials, yet conventional ab initio optimization remains a major bottleneck in high-throughput screening workflows. Machine learning potentials have accelerated relaxation by orders of magnitude, but they still rely on iterative optimization and high-quality DFT force labels. Here, we present HotRelax, a high-order tensor message-passing neural network for one-shot, end-to-end prediction of relaxed structures. Trained directly on paired unrelaxed and relaxed structures, HotRelax requires no DFT force labels and predicts relaxed structures in a single forward pass, without iterative inference or post-processing. Across five diverse datasets spanning 3D bulk crystals, 2D layered materials and catalysts, HotRelax shows strong performance relative to state-of-the-art end-to-end relaxation models, achieving lower prediction errors on several benchmarks while maintaining a compact model size and efficient inference. Extensive DFT calculations further show that the predicted structures are close in energy to their DFT-relaxed counterparts. When integrated into catalytic workflows, HotRelax also improves the accuracy and generalization of relaxed-state energy prediction models. Together, these results support HotRelax as an efficient and widely applicable framework for end-to-end structure relaxation, with strong potential to accelerate high-throughput materials discovery.

</details>


### [335] [Non-linear control variate in δf particle-in-cell methods using symplectic neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30622)
*Victor Fournet,Martin Campos Pinto,Emmanuel Franck,Victor Michel-Dansac*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合δf粒子网格法和辛神经网络的新方法，用于静电等离子体的动力学模拟，其中通过辛神经网络演化体密度作为控制变量，并引入了周期性辛神经网络架构，在1D1V和3D3V的Vlasov-Poisson系统上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统δf PIC方法中，体密度的演化存在数值误差累积问题，需要一种更精确的方式来逼近反向流，从而提高模拟的长期稳定性和精度。

Method: 将δf PIC方法与辛神经网络相结合：利用SympNets近似反向流，通过粒子轨迹训练网络；同时引入周期性SympNet架构以编码空间周期性。

Result: 在1D1V和3D3V的Vlasov-Poisson系统上进行了数值验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够利用辛神经网络的保结构特性改善δf PIC模拟的精度和稳定性，周期性架构有效处理了空间周期边界条件。

Abstract: We present a novel δf particle-in-cell (PIC) method for the kinetic simulation of electrostatic plasmas in which the bulk density, acting as a control variate, is evolved using symplectic neural networks (SympNets). The SympNets are used as an approximation of the backward flow and trained using the particle trajectories. We introduce a periodic variant of the SympNet architecture that encodes the spatial periodicity of the problem into the network itself. We validate the approach with numerical results in 1D1V and 3D3V for the Vlasov-Poisson system.

</details>
